FILE: src/app/secondary/evidence/page.tsx
================================================================================

import Link from "next/link";

import { DisclaimerBanner, DisclaimerFooter } from "@/components/Disclaimer";
import { indexById } from "@/lib/catalog/lookup";
import { loadConditionCatalog } from "@/lib/catalog/load";
import { recommend } from "@/lib/engine/recommend";
import { loadKnowledgeBase } from "@/lib/kb/load";
import { groupSuggestionsByPrimary } from "@/lib/secondary-workflow/suggestions";
import {
  parseRatingsSelection,
  parseWorkflowState,
  ratingFieldName,
  workflowStateToSearchParams,
} from "@/lib/secondary-workflow/state";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export default async function EvidenceReviewStepPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>>;
}) {
  const catalog = loadConditionCatalog();
  const kb = loadKnowledgeBase();
  const byId = indexById(catalog);
  const params = await searchParams;

  let state;
  let guardHref = "/secondary/diagnosed";
  let guardLabel = "Start over at diagnosed conditions";

  try {
    state = parseWorkflowState(params, catalog);
  } catch {
    try {
      const parsed = parseRatingsSelection(params, catalog);
      guardHref = `/secondary/upload?${new URLSearchParams([
        ...parsed.diagnosedConditionIds.map((id) => ["diagnosed", id]),
        ...parsed.serviceConnectedConditionIds.map((id) => ["serviceConnected", id]),
        ...parsed.serviceConnectedConditionIds.map((id) => [
          ratingFieldName(id),
          parsed.ratingsByConditionId[id] === null ? "decline" : String(parsed.ratingsByConditionId[id]),
        ]),
      ]).toString()}`;
      guardLabel = "Back to optional document upload";
    } catch {
      // Fall back to diagnosed step.
    }

    return (
      <main>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Complete prior steps first</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
          We need your full workflow selections, including the upload-step choice, before building
          an evidence checklist.
        </p>
        <Link href={guardHref} className="mt-5 inline-block text-sm underline hover:text-slate-900">
          {guardLabel}
        </Link>
      </main>
    );
  }

  const recommendationsResult = recommend(catalog, kb, {
    primaryConditions: state.serviceConnectedConditionIds,
    symptoms: [],
    medications: [],
    exposures: [],
    notes: "",
  });

  const grouped = groupSuggestionsByPrimary(
    catalog,
    state.serviceConnectedConditionIds,
    recommendationsResult.recommendations,
  );

  return (
    <main>
      <Link
        href={`/secondary/suggestions?${workflowStateToSearchParams(state).toString()}`}
        className="text-sm underline hover:text-slate-900"
      >
        Back to suggested secondary conditions
      </Link>

      <header className="mt-3 mb-6">
        <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Step 6 of 6</p>
        <h1 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Evidence review checklist</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-relaxed text-slate-600">
          This page is a structured gap review to help you organize evidence for follow-up with a
          provider or accredited representative. It does not parse records or determine eligibility.
        </p>
      </header>

      <div className="mb-6">
        <DisclaimerBanner />
      </div>

      <section className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
        <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Current workflow context</h2>
        <ul className="mt-3 space-y-1 text-sm text-slate-700">
          {state.serviceConnectedConditionIds.map((id) => {
            const condition = byId.get(id);
            if (!condition) return null;
            const rating = state.ratingsByConditionId[id];
            return (
              <li key={id}>
                <span className="font-medium text-slate-900">{condition.name}</span>
                <span className="text-slate-600"> — {rating === null ? "rating not provided" : `${rating}%`}</span>
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ul>
        <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-slate-700">
          Documents flagged: {state.documentsShared ? "Yes, user has documents to review manually" : "No"}
        </p>
      </section>

      <section className="mt-6 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
        <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Document processing limits</h2>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-relaxed text-slate-700">
          This MVP does not read C-files, parse uploaded records, or extract medical findings. Use
          this page as an organizer for manual review with your provider or representative.
        </p>
      </section>

      <section className="mt-6 space-y-6">
        <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Evidence gaps by primary condition</h2>
        {grouped.map((group) => (
          <article key={group.primaryId} className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
            <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">{group.primaryName}</h3>
            {group.recommendations.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-700">
                This primary condition is recognized, but no authored secondary mappings are
                published for it yet.
              </p>
            ) : (
              <ul className="mt-3 space-y-3 text-sm text-slate-700">
                {group.recommendations.map((recommendation) => (
                  <li key={`${group.primaryId}-${recommendation.conditionId}`}>
                    <p className="font-medium text-slate-900">{recommendation.condition}</p>
                    <ul className="mt-1 list-disc space-y-1 pl-5">
                      {recommendation.evidenceGaps.map((gap) => (
                        <li key={gap}>{gap}</li>
                      ))}
                    </ul>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            )}
          </article>
        ))}
      </section>

      <p className="mt-8 text-sm text-slate-600">
        Done with this run? You can start a new secondary workflow from the{" "}
        <Link href="/secondary" className="underline hover:text-slate-900">
          secondary overview
        </Link>
        .
      </p>

      <DisclaimerFooter />
    </main>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/app/secondary/ratings/page.tsx
================================================================================

import Link from "next/link";

import { DisclaimerBanner, DisclaimerFooter } from "@/components/Disclaimer";
import { SecondaryWorkflowHiddenFields } from "@/components/SecondaryWorkflowHiddenFields";
import { indexById } from "@/lib/catalog/lookup";
import { loadConditionCatalog } from "@/lib/catalog/load";
import {
  parseDiagnosedSelection,
  parseRatingsPrefill,
  parseServiceConnectedSelection,
  ratingFieldName,
} from "@/lib/secondary-workflow/state";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

const RATING_OPTIONS = Array.from({ length: 101 }, (_, index) => index);

export default async function RatingsStepPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>>;
}) {
  const catalog = loadConditionCatalog();
  const byId = indexById(catalog);
  const params = await searchParams;

  let diagnosedConditionIds: string[];
  let serviceConnectedConditionIds: string[];
  let guardHref = "/secondary/diagnosed";
  let guardLabel = "Start over at diagnosed conditions";

  try {
    const parsed = parseServiceConnectedSelection(params, catalog);
    diagnosedConditionIds = parsed.diagnosedConditionIds;
    serviceConnectedConditionIds = parsed.serviceConnectedConditionIds;
  } catch {
    try {
      const diagnosed = parseDiagnosedSelection(params, catalog);
      guardHref = `/secondary/service-connected?${new URLSearchParams(
        diagnosed.diagnosedConditionIds.map((id) => ["diagnosed", id]),
      ).toString()}`;
      guardLabel = "Back to service-connected status";
    } catch {
      // Fall back to diagnosed step.
    }

    return (
      <main>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Set service-connected status first</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
          Mark at least one condition as already service connected before entering ratings.
        </p>
        <Link href={guardHref} className="mt-5 inline-block text-sm underline hover:text-slate-900">
          {guardLabel}
        </Link>
      </main>
    );
  }

  const serviceConnected = serviceConnectedConditionIds
    .map((id) => byId.get(id))
    .filter((condition): condition is NonNullable<typeof condition> => condition !== undefined);
  const ratingsByConditionId = parseRatingsPrefill(params, serviceConnectedConditionIds);

  return (
    <main>
      <Link
        href={`/secondary/service-connected?${new URLSearchParams([
          ...diagnosedConditionIds.map((id) => ["diagnosed", id]),
          ...serviceConnectedConditionIds.map((id) => ["serviceConnected", id]),
        ]).toString()}`}
        className="text-sm underline hover:text-slate-900"
      >
        Back to service-connected status
      </Link>

      <header className="mt-3 mb-6">
        <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Step 3 of 6</p>
        <h1 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Enter current ratings</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-relaxed text-slate-600">
          Ratings are optional context for your checklist. You can decline to answer for any
          condition.
        </p>
      </header>

      <div className="mb-6">
        <DisclaimerBanner />
      </div>

      <form action="/secondary/upload" method="get" className="space-y-4">
        <SecondaryWorkflowHiddenFields
          diagnosedConditionIds={diagnosedConditionIds}
          serviceConnectedConditionIds={serviceConnectedConditionIds}
        />

        {serviceConnected.map((condition) => (
          <label
            key={condition.id}
            className="block rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white p-4 shadow-sm"
          >
            <span className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">{condition.name}</span>
            <span className="block text-xs text-slate-500">{condition.bodySystem}</span>

            <select
              name={ratingFieldName(condition.id)}
              className="mt-3 w-full rounded-md border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
              defaultValue={
                ratingsByConditionId[condition.id] === null
                  ? "decline"
                  : String(ratingsByConditionId[condition.id])
              }
            >
              <option value="decline">I prefer not to answer</option>
              {RATING_OPTIONS.map((rating) => (
                <option key={rating} value={rating}>
                  {rating}%
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </label>
        ))}

        <button
          type="submit"
          className="rounded-md bg-slate-900 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-slate-700"
        >
          Continue to optional document upload
        </button>
      </form>

      <DisclaimerFooter />
    </main>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/app/secondary/service-connected/page.tsx
================================================================================

import Link from "next/link";

import { DisclaimerBanner, DisclaimerFooter } from "@/components/Disclaimer";
import { SecondaryWorkflowHiddenFields } from "@/components/SecondaryWorkflowHiddenFields";
import { indexById } from "@/lib/catalog/lookup";
import { loadConditionCatalog } from "@/lib/catalog/load";
import {
  parseDiagnosedSelection,
  parseServiceConnectedPrefill,
} from "@/lib/secondary-workflow/state";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export default async function ServiceConnectedStepPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>>;
}) {
  const catalog = loadConditionCatalog();
  const byId = indexById(catalog);
  const params = await searchParams;

  let diagnosedConditionIds: string[];
  let selectedServiceConnectedIds = new Set<string>();
  let hasPriorSelection = false;

  try {
    diagnosedConditionIds = parseDiagnosedSelection(params, catalog).diagnosedConditionIds;
  } catch {
    return (
      <main>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Select diagnosed conditions first</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
          Choose at least one diagnosed condition before setting service-connected status.
        </p>
        <Link href="/secondary/diagnosed" className="mt-5 inline-block text-sm underline hover:text-slate-900">
          Back to diagnosed conditions
        </Link>
      </main>
    );
  }

  selectedServiceConnectedIds = new Set(
    parseServiceConnectedPrefill(params, catalog).serviceConnectedConditionIds,
  );
  hasPriorSelection =
    selectedServiceConnectedIds.size > 0 ||
    params.serviceConnected !== undefined ||
    diagnosedConditionIds.some((id) => params[`serviceConnected-${id}`] !== undefined);

  const diagnosed = diagnosedConditionIds
    .map((id) => byId.get(id))
    .filter((condition): condition is NonNullable<typeof condition> => condition !== undefined);

  return (
    <main>
      <Link
        href={`/secondary/diagnosed?${new URLSearchParams(
          diagnosedConditionIds.map((id) => ["diagnosed", id]),
        ).toString()}`}
        className="text-sm underline hover:text-slate-900"
      >
        Back to diagnosed conditions
      </Link>

      <header className="mt-3 mb-6">
        <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Step 2 of 6</p>
        <h1 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Mark service-connected status</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-relaxed text-slate-600">
          For each diagnosed condition you selected, mark whether VA has already service connected
          it.
        </p>
      </header>

      <div className="mb-6">
        <DisclaimerBanner />
      </div>

      <form action="/secondary/ratings" method="get" className="space-y-4">
        <SecondaryWorkflowHiddenFields diagnosedConditionIds={diagnosedConditionIds} />

        {diagnosed.map((condition) => (
          <fieldset
            key={condition.id}
            className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white p-4 shadow-sm"
          >
            <legend className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">{condition.name}</legend>
            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">{condition.bodySystem}</p>

            <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-4 text-sm text-slate-700">
              <label className="inline-flex items-center gap-2">
                <input
                  type="radio"
                  name={`serviceConnected-${condition.id}`}
                  value="yes"
                  defaultChecked={
                    hasPriorSelection
                      ? selectedServiceConnectedIds.has(condition.id)
                      : true
                  }
                  className="h-4 w-4 border-slate-300"
                />
                Yes, already service connected
              </label>
              <label className="inline-flex items-center gap-2">
                <input
                  type="radio"
                  name={`serviceConnected-${condition.id}`}
                  value="no"
                  defaultChecked={
                    hasPriorSelection
                      ? !selectedServiceConnectedIds.has(condition.id)
                      : false
                  }
                  className="h-4 w-4 border-slate-300"
                />
                No, not service connected
              </label>
            </div>
          </fieldset>
        ))}

        <button
          type="submit"
          className="rounded-md bg-slate-900 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-slate-700"
        >
          Continue to current ratings
        </button>
      </form>

      <DisclaimerFooter />
    </main>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/app/secondary/suggestions/page.tsx
================================================================================

import Link from "next/link";

import { DisclaimerBanner, DisclaimerFooter } from "@/components/Disclaimer";
import {
  SecondaryWorkflowHiddenFields,
  toHiddenFieldState,
} from "@/components/SecondaryWorkflowHiddenFields";
import { SecondarySuggestionItem } from "@/components/SecondarySuggestionItem";
import { loadConditionCatalog } from "@/lib/catalog/load";
import { recommend } from "@/lib/engine/recommend";
import { loadKnowledgeBase } from "@/lib/kb/load";
import { groupSuggestionsByPrimary } from "@/lib/secondary-workflow/suggestions";
import {
  parseRatingsSelection,
  parseWorkflowState,
  ratingFieldName,
  workflowStateToSearchParams,
} from "@/lib/secondary-workflow/state";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export default async function SuggestionsStepPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>>;
}) {
  const catalog = loadConditionCatalog();
  const kb = loadKnowledgeBase();
  const params = await searchParams;

  let state;
  let guardHref = "/secondary/diagnosed";
  let guardLabel = "Start over at diagnosed conditions";

  try {
    state = parseWorkflowState(params, catalog);
  } catch {
    try {
      const parsed = parseRatingsSelection(params, catalog);
      guardHref = `/secondary/upload?${new URLSearchParams([
        ...parsed.diagnosedConditionIds.map((id) => ["diagnosed", id]),
        ...parsed.serviceConnectedConditionIds.map((id) => ["serviceConnected", id]),
        ...parsed.serviceConnectedConditionIds.map((id) => [
          ratingFieldName(id),
          parsed.ratingsByConditionId[id] === null ? "decline" : String(parsed.ratingsByConditionId[id]),
        ]),
      ]).toString()}`;
      guardLabel = "Back to optional document upload";
    } catch {
      // Fall back to diagnosed step.
    }

    return (
      <main>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Complete prior steps first</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
          We need your diagnosed conditions, service-connected selections, ratings context, and
          upload-step choice before showing grouped suggestions.
        </p>
        <Link href={guardHref} className="mt-5 inline-block text-sm underline hover:text-slate-900">
          {guardLabel}
        </Link>
      </main>
    );
  }

  const recommendationsResult = recommend(catalog, kb, {
    primaryConditions: state.serviceConnectedConditionIds,
    symptoms: [],
    medications: [],
    exposures: [],
    notes: "",
  });

  const grouped = groupSuggestionsByPrimary(
    catalog,
    state.serviceConnectedConditionIds,
    recommendationsResult.recommendations,
  );

  return (
    <main>
      <Link
        href={`/secondary/upload?${workflowStateToSearchParams(state).toString()}`}
        className="text-sm underline hover:text-slate-900"
      >
        Back to optional document upload
      </Link>

      <header className="mt-3 mb-6">
        <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Step 5 of 6</p>
        <h1 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Suggested secondary conditions</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-relaxed text-slate-600">
          Suggestions are grouped by each service-connected primary condition you selected. These
          are possibilities to explore, not determinations. Draft mappings are excluded.
        </p>
      </header>

      <div className="mb-6">
        <DisclaimerBanner />
      </div>

      {recommendationsResult.unmappedPrimaries.length > 0 && (
        <section className="mb-6 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white p-4 text-sm text-slate-700">
          <h2 className="font-semibold text-slate-900">Recognized, but not yet mapped</h2>
          <p className="mt-1 leading-relaxed">
            {recommendationsResult.unmappedPrimaries.join(", ")} {recommendationsResult.unmappedPrimaries.length === 1 ? "is" : "are"} in the catalog, but no
            authored secondary mappings are published yet. Their absence here does not mean a
            secondary claim is impossible.
          </p>
        </section>
      )}

      {grouped.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 text-sm text-slate-700">
          No authored secondary mappings were found for the currently selected service-connected
          conditions.
        </p>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-6">
          {grouped.map((group) => (
            <section key={group.primaryId} className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
              <h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">{group.primaryName}</h2>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                Service-connected primary condition
              </p>
              {group.recommendations.length === 0 ? (
                <p className="mt-3 rounded-md bg-slate-50 p-4 text-sm text-slate-700">
                  This condition is recognized, but no authored secondary mappings are published for
                  it yet.
                </p>
              ) : (
                <div className="mt-4 space-y-3">
                  {group.recommendations.map((recommendation) => (
                    <SecondarySuggestionItem
                      key={`${group.primaryId}-${recommendation.conditionId}`}
                      recommendation={recommendation}
                      primaryId={group.primaryId}
                    />
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </section>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      <form action="/secondary/evidence" method="get" className="mt-8">
        <SecondaryWorkflowHiddenFields {...toHiddenFieldState(state)} />
        <button
          type="submit"
          className="rounded-md bg-slate-900 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-slate-700"
        >
          Continue to evidence review
        </button>
      </form>

      <DisclaimerFooter />
    </main>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/app/secondary/upload/page.tsx
================================================================================

import Link from "next/link";

import { DisclaimerBanner, DisclaimerFooter } from "@/components/Disclaimer";
import { SecondaryWorkflowHiddenFields } from "@/components/SecondaryWorkflowHiddenFields";
import { indexById } from "@/lib/catalog/lookup";
import { loadConditionCatalog } from "@/lib/catalog/load";
import {
  parseDocumentsSharedPrefill,
  parseRatingsSelection,
  parseServiceConnectedSelection,
  ratingFieldName,
} from "@/lib/secondary-workflow/state";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export default async function UploadStepPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>>;
}) {
  const catalog = loadConditionCatalog();
  const byId = indexById(catalog);
  const params = await searchParams;

  let diagnosedConditionIds: string[];
  let serviceConnectedConditionIds: string[];
  let ratingsByConditionId: Record<string, number | null>;
  const documentsShared = parseDocumentsSharedPrefill(params.documentsShared);
  let guardHref = "/secondary/diagnosed";
  let guardLabel = "Start over at diagnosed conditions";

  try {
    const parsed = parseRatingsSelection(params, catalog);
    diagnosedConditionIds = parsed.diagnosedConditionIds;
    serviceConnectedConditionIds = parsed.serviceConnectedConditionIds;
    ratingsByConditionId = parsed.ratingsByConditionId;
  } catch {
    try {
      const parsed = parseServiceConnectedSelection(params, catalog);
      guardHref = `/secondary/ratings?${new URLSearchParams([
        ...parsed.diagnosedConditionIds.map((id) => ["diagnosed", id]),
        ...parsed.serviceConnectedConditionIds.map((id) => ["serviceConnected", id]),
      ]).toString()}`;
      guardLabel = "Back to current ratings";
    } catch {
      // Fall back to diagnosed step.
    }

    return (
      <main>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Enter ratings first</h1>
<div className="my-4 p-4 bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 rounded-lg text-xs text-blue-900">
    <span className="font-semibold">🔒 Evidence Upload Privacy Contract:</span> Uploading documents is strictly optional. Files are processed locally or through secure non-retentive extraction boundaries. We do <span className="underline font-semibold">not</span> store raw text logs, medical record files, or personal identifiers (PII).
  </div>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
          Complete the ratings step before you continue to document upload.
        </p>
        <Link href={guardHref} className="mt-5 inline-block text-sm underline hover:text-slate-900">
          {guardLabel}
        </Link>
      </main>
    );
  }

  return (
    <main>
      <Link
        href={`/secondary/ratings?${new URLSearchParams([
          ...diagnosedConditionIds.map((id) => ["diagnosed", id]),
          ...serviceConnectedConditionIds.map((id) => ["serviceConnected", id]),
          ...serviceConnectedConditionIds.map((id) => [
            ratingFieldName(id),
            ratingsByConditionId[id] === null ? "decline" : String(ratingsByConditionId[id]),
          ]),
        ]).toString()}`}
        className="text-sm underline hover:text-slate-900"
      >
        Back to current ratings
      </Link>

      <header className="mt-3 mb-6">
        <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Step 4 of 6</p>
        <h1 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Optional document upload</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-relaxed text-slate-600">
          Uploads are optional. Use this step only if you want a reminder in your checklist that
          you have records to review manually with your provider or representative.
        </p>
      </header>

      <div className="mb-6">
        <DisclaimerBanner />
      </div>

      <section className="mb-5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white p-4 text-sm text-slate-700">
        <h2 className="font-semibold text-slate-900">What this step does today</h2>
        <ul className="mt-2 list-disc space-y-1 pl-5">
          <li>Helps you prepare a document checklist for your next representative meeting.</li>
          <li>Does not medically interpret, summarize, or rate what is inside your files.</li>
          <li>Does not save document text in event logs or user accounts.</li>
        </ul>
      </section>

      <form action="/secondary/suggestions" method="get" className="space-y-6">
        <SecondaryWorkflowHiddenFields
          diagnosedConditionIds={diagnosedConditionIds}
          serviceConnectedConditionIds={serviceConnectedConditionIds}
          ratingsByConditionId={ratingsByConditionId}
        />

        <label className="block rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white p-4 shadow-sm">
          <span className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">Optional local file picker</span>
          <span className="mt-1 block text-xs text-slate-500">
            Files selected here are not uploaded for AI analysis in this Phase 1 MVP.
          </span>
          <input
            type="file"
            multiple
            className="mt-3 block w-full text-sm text-slate-700 file:mr-3 file:rounded-md file:border-0 file:bg-slate-900 file:px-3 file:py-2 file:text-sm file:font-medium file:text-white"
            aria-describedby="upload-processing-limit"
          />
        </label>

        <fieldset>
          <legend className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">
            Do you want this reflected in your checklist?
          </legend>
          <p id="upload-processing-limit" className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600">
            This choice only updates checklist wording. It does not trigger document parsing.
          </p>
          <div className="mt-3 space-y-2 text-sm text-slate-700">
            <label className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <input
                type="radio"
                name="documentsShared"
                value="yes"
                defaultChecked={documentsShared}
                className="h-4 w-4"
              />
              Yes, I have documents ready to review manually
            </label>
            <label className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <input
                type="radio"
                name="documentsShared"
                value="no"
                defaultChecked={!documentsShared}
                className="h-4 w-4"
              />
              No, continue without documents
            </label>
          </div>
        </fieldset>

        <section className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white p-4 text-sm text-slate-700">
          <h2 className="font-semibold text-slate-900">Your service-connected conditions in this run</h2>
          <ul className="mt-2 space-y-1">
            {serviceConnectedConditionIds.map((id) => {
              const condition = byId.get(id);
              if (!condition) return null;
              const rating = ratingsByConditionId[id];
              return (
                <li key={id}>
                  <span className="font-medium text-slate-900">{condition.name}</span>
                  <span className="text-slate-600"> — {rating === null ? "rating not provided" : `${rating}%`}</span>
                </li>
              );
            })}
          </ul>
        </section>

        <button
          type="submit"
          className="rounded-md bg-slate-900 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-slate-700"
        >
          Continue to suggested secondary conditions
        </button>
      </form>

      <DisclaimerFooter />
    </main>
  );
}

import React from "react";
import Link from "next/link";

export default function TermsPage() {
  return (
    <main className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 py-8 text-gray-900">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-4">Terms of Use & Legal Disclaimers</h1>
      <p className="text-sm text-gray-600 mb-6">Last updated: July 2026</p>
      
      <section className="space-y-4 text-sm leading-relaxed">
        <div className="bg-amber-50 border-l-4 border-amber-600 p-4 rounded">
          <h2 className="font-semibold text-amber-900">Not Legal or Medical Advice</h2>
          <p className="text-amber-800 text-xs mt-1">

          </p>
        </div>

        <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold mt-6">1. Regulatory Path Distinction</h2>
        <p>
          Users must distinguish between legally distinct claim paths:
        </p>
        <ul className="list-disc pl-6 space-y-1 text-xs">
          <li><strong>Secondary Service Connection (38 CFR 3.310):</strong> Requires an established primary service-connected disability and formal medical nexus evidence.</li>
          <li><strong>Presumptive Service Connection (38 CFR 3.307 / 3.309):</strong> Based on qualifying service dates/locations (e.g., PACT Act airborne hazards) without requiring a direct nexus opinion.</li>
        </ul>

        <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold mt-6">2. Citation Verification Notice</h2>
        <p>
          All regulatory references (such as 38 CFR citations) are provided as unverified starting points (<code className="bg-gray-100 px-1 rounded">verified: false</code>) requiring verification by a qualified Veterans Service Officer (VSO) or accredited representative.
        </p>

        <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold mt-6">3. Knowledge Base Completeness</h2>
        <p>
          The VASRD catalog contains 191 conditions across 14 body systems. Published secondary mappings actively return exploration suggestions, while draft placeholders are strictly excluded from recommendation results. Absence of a published mapping does not imply a secondary connection is legally invalid.
        </p>
      </section>

      <div className="mt-8 pt-4 border-t">
        <Link href="/" className="text-blue-600 hover:underline text-sm font-medium">
          &larr; Return to Home
        </Link>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

import React from "react";

interface DataProvenanceProps {
  status: "published" | "draft" | "unmapped";
  conditionName: string;
}

export const DataProvenanceBadge: React.FC<DataProvenanceProps> = ({ status, conditionName }) => {
  if (status === "published") {
    return (
      <div className="p-3 my-2 bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 rounded-md text-sm text-blue-900">
        <span className="font-semibold">Source Provenance:</span> Local Knowledge Base (Published & Research-Ready)
        <p className="text-xs text-blue-700 mt-1">
          Citations ship with <code className="bg-blue-100 px-1 py-0.5 rounded">verified: false</code> requiring formal human review.
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-4 my-3 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-300 rounded-md text-sm text-amber-900">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-semibold text-amber-950">
        <span className="inline-block w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-amber-500"></span>
        Recognized Condition — Secondary Mapping Research in Progress
      </div>
      <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-amber-800 leading-relaxed">
        <strong>{conditionName}</strong> exists in the VASRD catalog, but authored secondary mappings are currently in draft status. 
        Absence of published mappings in this research tool does <em>not</em> imply that a secondary connection under 38 CFR 3.310 is impossible.
      </p>
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: src/components/IntakeForm.tsx

"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";

export interface IntakeOption {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  bodySystem: string;
  aliases: string[];
  /** True when authored secondary-condition research exists for this entry. */
  hasResearch: boolean;
}

interface Props {
  options: IntakeOption[];
}

const fieldClass =
  "w-full rounded-md border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm shadow-sm focus:border-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-slate-900";

const MAX_VISIBLE = 40;

export function IntakeForm({ options }: Props) {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<IntakeOption[]>([]);
  const [researchOnly, setResearchOnly] = useState(true);

  const selectedIds = useMemo(
    () => new Set(selected.map((option) => option.id)),
    [selected],
  );

  const matches = useMemo(() => {
    const needle = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    return options
      .filter((option) => !selectedIds.has(option.id))
      .filter((option) => (researchOnly ? option.hasResearch : true))
      .filter((option) => {
        if (!needle) return true;
        return (
          option.name.toLowerCase().includes(needle) ||
          option.bodySystem.toLowerCase().includes(needle) ||
          option.aliases.some((alias) => alias.toLowerCase().includes(needle))
        );
      });
  }, [options, query, researchOnly, selectedIds]);

  const visible = matches.slice(0, MAX_VISIBLE);
  const researchCount = options.filter((option) => option.hasResearch).length;

  return (
    <form action="/results" method="get" className="space-y-8">
      <fieldset>
        <legend className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">
          Conditions you are already rated for
        </legend>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600">
          Search {options.length} conditions and select every one you are
          service connected for.
        </p>

        {selected.length > 0 && (
          <ul className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {selected.map((option) => (
              <li key={option.id}>
                <input type="hidden" name="primary" value={option.id} />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() =>
                    setSelected((current) =>
                      current.filter((item) => item.id !== option.id),
                    )
                  }
                  className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-slate-900 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm text-white hover:bg-slate-700"
                >
                  {option.name}
                  <span aria-hidden className="text-slate-300">
                    &times;
                  </span>
                  <span className="sr-only">Remove {option.name}</span>
                </button>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        )}

        <input
          type="search"
          value={query}
          onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
          placeholder="Search by condition or body system"
          aria-label="Search conditions"
          className={`mt-3 ${fieldClass}`}
        />

        <label className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-slate-700">
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            checked={researchOnly}
            onChange={(event) => setResearchOnly(event.target.checked)}
            className="h-4 w-4 rounded border-slate-300"
          />
          Only show the {researchCount} conditions with secondary research
          written
        </label>

        <ul className="mt-3 max-h-80 divide-y divide-slate-100 overflow-y-auto rounded-md border border-slate-200 bg-white">
          {visible.length === 0 && (
            <li className="px-3 py-4 text-sm text-slate-600">
              No conditions match that search.
            </li>
          )}
          {visible.map((option) => (
            <li key={option.id}>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setSelected((current) => [...current, option])}
                className="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 text-left text-sm hover:bg-slate-50"
              >
                <span>
                  {option.name}
                  <span className="mt-0.5 block text-xs text-slate-500">
                    {option.bodySystem}
                  </span>
                </span>
                {option.hasResearch ? (
                  <span className="shrink-0 rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs text-slate-700">
                    research available
                  </span>
                ) : (
                  <span className="shrink-0 rounded-full bg-amber-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs text-amber-800">
                    no mappings yet
                  </span>
                )}
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>

        {matches.length > visible.length && (
          <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-slate-500">
            Showing {visible.length} of {matches.length}. Keep typing to narrow
            the list.
          </p>
        )}
      </fieldset>

      <fieldset className="space-y-4">
        <legend className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">
          Optional context
        </legend>
        <p className="-mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600">
          Used to highlight relevant details in your results. It does not change
          which suggestions appear or how they are ordered.
        </p>

        <label className="block">
          <span className="text-sm text-slate-700">
            Symptoms <span className="text-slate-400">(comma separated)</span>
          </span>
          <input
            className={`mt-1 ${fieldClass}`}
            name="symptoms"
            placeholder="trouble sleeping, daytime fatigue"
          />
        </label>

        <label className="block">
          <span className="text-sm text-slate-700">
            Medications <span className="text-slate-400">(comma separated)</span>
          </span>
          <input
            className={`mt-1 ${fieldClass}`}
            name="medications"
            placeholder="sertraline, meloxicam"
          />
        </label>

        <label className="block">
          <span className="text-sm text-slate-700">
            Exposures <span className="text-slate-400">(comma separated)</span>
          </span>
          <input
            className={`mt-1 ${fieldClass}`}
            name="exposures"
            placeholder="burn pits, aircraft engine noise"
          />
        </label>

        <label className="block">
          <span className="text-sm text-slate-700">Notes</span>
          <textarea
            className={`mt-1 ${fieldClass}`}
            name="notes"
            rows={3}
            maxLength={2000}
            placeholder="Anything else that may be relevant."
          />
        </label>
      </fieldset>

      <button
        type="submit"
        disabled={selected.length === 0}
        className="w-full rounded-md bg-slate-900 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-slate-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-slate-300 sm:w-auto"
      >
        Show conditions to explore
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/SecondarySuggestionItem.tsx

import type { Recommendation } from "@/lib/engine/types";

const PREVALENCE_LABEL: Record<Recommendation["prevalence"], string> = {
  "commonly-explored": "Commonly explored",
  "sometimes-explored": "Sometimes explored",
  "occasionally-explored": "Occasionally explored",
};

interface Props {
  recommendation: Recommendation;
  primaryId: string;
}

export function SecondarySuggestionItem({ recommendation, primaryId }: Props) {
  const primaryContext = recommendation.contributingPrimaries.find(
    (primary) => primary.id === primaryId,
  );

  return (
    <article className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white p-4 shadow-sm">
      <header className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Possible secondary condition</p>
          <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">{recommendation.condition}</h3>
        </div>
        <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-2 py-1 text-xs text-slate-700">
          {PREVALENCE_LABEL[recommendation.prevalence]}
        </span>
      </header>

      {primaryContext && (
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-700">
          <span className="font-medium text-slate-900">Service-connected primary: </span>
          {primaryContext.name}
        </p>
      )}

      {primaryContext && (
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-relaxed text-slate-700">
          <span className="font-medium text-slate-900">Why this may be worth exploring: </span>
          {primaryContext.rationale}
        </p>
      )}

      <section className="mt-3 rounded-md bg-slate-50 p-3">
        <h4 className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
          Evidence that may help
        </h4>
        <ul className="mt-1.5 list-disc space-y-1 pl-5 text-sm text-slate-700">
          {recommendation.evidenceGaps.map((gap) => (
            <li key={gap}>{gap}</li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </section>

      {recommendation.sourceReferences.length > 0 && (
        <section className="mt-3">
          <h4 className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
            Mapped sources
          </h4>
          <ul className="mt-1.5 space-y-1.5">
            {recommendation.sourceReferences.slice(0, 3).map((reference) => (
              <li key={reference.citation} className="text-sm text-slate-700">
                <span className="font-mono text-xs text-slate-900">{reference.citation}</span>
                {!reference.verified && (
                  <span className="ml-2 rounded bg-amber-100 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs text-amber-900">
                    unverified
                  </span>
                )}
                <span className="mt-0.5 block text-xs text-slate-500">{reference.note}</span>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </section>
      )}
    </article>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/components/SecondaryWorkflowHiddenFields.tsx
================================================================================

import { ratingFieldName, type SecondaryWorkflowState } from "@/lib/secondary-workflow/state";

interface Props {
  diagnosedConditionIds: string[];
  serviceConnectedConditionIds?: string[];
  ratingsByConditionId?: Record<string, number | null>;
  documentsShared?: boolean;
}

export function SecondaryWorkflowHiddenFields({
  diagnosedConditionIds,
  serviceConnectedConditionIds = [],
  ratingsByConditionId,
  documentsShared,
}: Props) {
  return (
    <>
      {diagnosedConditionIds.map((conditionId) => (
        <input key={`diagnosed-${conditionId}`} type="hidden" name="diagnosed" value={conditionId} />
      ))}

      {serviceConnectedConditionIds.map((conditionId) => (
        <input
          key={`service-connected-${conditionId}`}
          type="hidden"
          name="serviceConnected"
          value={conditionId}
        />
      ))}

      {ratingsByConditionId &&
        serviceConnectedConditionIds.map((conditionId) => (
          <input
            key={`rating-${conditionId}`}
            type="hidden"
            name={ratingFieldName(conditionId)}
            value={
              ratingsByConditionId[conditionId] === null
                ? "decline"
                : String(ratingsByConditionId[conditionId] ?? "decline")
            }
          />
        ))}

      {typeof documentsShared === "boolean" && (
        <input
          type="hidden"
          name="documentsShared"
          value={documentsShared ? "yes" : "no"}
        />
      )}
    </>
  );
}

export function toHiddenFieldState(state: SecondaryWorkflowState): Props {
  return {
    diagnosedConditionIds: state.diagnosedConditionIds,
    serviceConnectedConditionIds: state.serviceConnectedConditionIds,
    ratingsByConditionId: state.ratingsByConditionId,
    documentsShared: state.documentsShared,
  };
}

FILE: src/components/ServiceHistoryForm.tsx

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";

interface Props {
  locations: string[];
}

const fieldClass =
  "w-full rounded-md border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm shadow-sm focus:border-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-slate-900";

export function ServiceHistoryForm({ locations }: Props) {
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<string[]>([]);

  function toggle(location: string) {
    setSelected((current) =>
      current.includes(location)
        ? current.filter((item) => item !== location)
        : [...current, location],
    );
  }

  return (
    <form action="/presumptive/results" method="get" className="space-y-8">
      <fieldset>
        <legend className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">
          Where did you serve?
        </legend>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600">
          Select every location. Presumptive eligibility depends on location and
          dates, not on any condition you are already service connected for.
        </p>

        <div className="mt-3 grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2">
          {locations.map((location) => (
            <label
              key={location}
              className="flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-3 rounded-md border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm hover:border-slate-400"
            >
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                name="location"
                value={location}
                checked={selected.includes(location)}
                onChange={() => toggle(location)}
                className="h-4 w-4 rounded border-slate-300"
              />
              <span>{location}</span>
            </label>
          ))}
        </div>
      </fieldset>

      <fieldset>
        <legend className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">
          When did you serve there?
        </legend>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600">
          Optional, but without dates the results cannot confirm you fall inside
          a qualifying window. Check your DD-214 for exact dates.
        </p>

        <div className="mt-3 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <label className="block">
            <span className="text-sm text-slate-700">Start date</span>
            <input type="date" name="serviceStart" className={`mt-1 ${fieldClass}`} />
          </label>
          <label className="block">
            <span className="text-sm text-slate-700">End date</span>
            <input type="date" name="serviceEnd" className={`mt-1 ${fieldClass}`} />
          </label>
        </div>
      </fieldset>

      <button
        type="submit"
        disabled={selected.length === 0}
        className="w-full rounded-md bg-slate-900 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-slate-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-slate-300 sm:w-auto"
      >
        Show presumptive conditions
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/components/SuggestionCard.tsx
================================================================================

import type { Recommendation } from "@/lib/engine/types";

const PREVALENCE_LABEL: Record<Recommendation["prevalence"], string> = {
  "commonly-explored": "Commonly explored",
  "sometimes-explored": "Sometimes explored",
  "occasionally-explored": "Occasionally explored",
};

const PREVALENCE_STYLE: Record<Recommendation["prevalence"], string> = {
  "commonly-explored": "bg-slate-900 text-white",
  "sometimes-explored": "bg-slate-200 text-slate-800",
  "occasionally-explored": "bg-slate-100 text-slate-600",
};

export function SuggestionCard({
  recommendation,
  position,
}: {
  recommendation: Recommendation;
  position: number;
}) {
  const unverified = recommendation.sourceReferences.some(
    (reference) => !reference.verified,
  );

  return (
    <article className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
      <header className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">
          {position}. {recommendation.condition}
        </h3>
        <span
          className={`rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium ${PREVALENCE_STYLE[recommendation.prevalence]}`}
        >
          {PREVALENCE_LABEL[recommendation.prevalence]}
        </span>
      </header>

      <section className="mt-4">
        <h4 className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
          Why this may be relevant
        </h4>
        <ul className="mt-2 space-y-2">
          {recommendation.contributingPrimaries.map((primary) => (
            <li key={primary.id} className="text-sm leading-relaxed text-slate-700">
              <span className="font-medium text-slate-900">{primary.name}:</span>{" "}
              {primary.rationale}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </section>

      {recommendation.matchedIntakeContext.length > 0 && (
        <section className="mt-4">
          <h4 className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
            Context you provided
          </h4>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-700">
            {recommendation.matchedIntakeContext.join(", ")}
          </p>
        </section>
      )}

      <section className="mt-4 rounded-md bg-slate-50 p-4">
        <h4 className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
          What a claim on this theory typically still needs
        </h4>
        <ul className="mt-2 list-disc space-y-1 pl-5 text-sm text-slate-700">
          {recommendation.evidenceGaps.map((gap) => (
            <li key={gap}>{gap}</li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </section>

      <section className="mt-4">
        <h4 className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
          Starting points for verification
        </h4>
        <ul className="mt-2 space-y-1.5">
          {recommendation.sourceReferences.map((reference) => (
            <li key={reference.citation} className="text-sm text-slate-700">
              <span className="font-mono text-xs text-slate-900">
                {reference.citation}
              </span>
              {!reference.verified && (
                <span className="ml-2 rounded bg-amber-100 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs text-amber-900">
                  unverified
                </span>
              )}
              <span className="mt-0.5 block text-xs text-slate-500">
                {reference.note}
              </span>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
        {unverified && (
          <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-amber-800">
            Citations marked unverified have not been confirmed by a qualified
            reviewer. Check them against current VA guidance before relying on
            them.
          </p>
        )}
      </section>
    </article>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/lib/catalog/load.ts
================================================================================

import "server-only";

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

import {
  catalogFileSchema,
  conditionCatalogSchema,
  type CatalogCondition,
  type ConditionCatalog,
} from "./schema";

const CATALOG_DIR = path.join(process.cwd(), "data", "conditions");

let cached: ConditionCatalog | null = null;

/** Reads and validates every catalog file. Duplicate ids are a hard error. */
export function loadConditionCatalog(): ConditionCatalog {
  if (cached) return cached;

  const files = fs
    .readdirSync(CATALOG_DIR)
    .filter((file) => file.endsWith(".json"))
    .sort();

  const conditions: CatalogCondition[] = [];
  const seen = new Map<string, string>();

  for (const file of files) {
    const raw = fs.readFileSync(path.join(CATALOG_DIR, file), "utf8");
    const parsed = catalogFileSchema.safeParse(JSON.parse(raw));

    if (!parsed.success) {
      throw new Error(`Invalid catalog file ${file}: ${parsed.error.message}`);
    }

    for (const condition of parsed.data.conditions) {
      const previous = seen.get(condition.id);
      if (previous) {
        throw new Error(
          `Duplicate condition id "${condition.id}" in ${file}; already defined in ${previous}.`,
        );
      }
      seen.set(condition.id, file);
      conditions.push(condition);
    }
  }

  cached = conditionCatalogSchema.parse(conditions);
  return cached;
}

/** Test seam: clears the module-level cache. */
export function resetConditionCatalogCache(): void {
  cached = null;
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/lib/catalog/lookup.ts
================================================================================

import type { CatalogCondition, ConditionCatalog } from "./schema";

/** Collapses case, trims, and squeezes internal whitespace. */
export function normalizeConditionName(condition: string): string {
  return condition.trim().toLowerCase().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).join(" ");
}

/** Id-keyed index for resolving display names without repeated scans. */
export function indexById(
  catalog: ConditionCatalog,
): Map<string, CatalogCondition> {
  return new Map(catalog.map((condition) => [condition.id, condition]));
}

/**
 * Resolves free-text input to a catalog condition by id, name, or alias.
 * Exact match only. Fuzzy matching is deliberately out of scope: silently
 * resolving "knee" to the wrong entry is worse than returning nothing.
 */
export function findCondition(
  catalog: ConditionCatalog,
  input: string,
): CatalogCondition | null {
  const normalized = normalizeConditionName(input);
  if (!normalized) return null;

  return (
    catalog.find((condition) => {
      const candidates = [
        condition.id,
        condition.id.replace(/-/g, " "),
        condition.name,
        ...condition.aliases,
      ];
      return candidates.some(
        (candidate) => normalizeConditionName(candidate) === normalized,
      );
    }) ?? null
  );
}

/** Conditions grouped by body system, both sorted for stable display. */
export function groupByBodySystem(
  catalog: ConditionCatalog,
): [string, CatalogCondition[]][] {
  const groups = new Map<string, CatalogCondition[]>();
  for (const condition of catalog) {
    groups.set(condition.bodySystem, [
      ...(groups.get(condition.bodySystem) ?? []),
      condition,
    ]);
  }
  return [...groups.entries()]
    .map(
      ([system, items]) =>
        [system, [...items].sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name))] as [
          string,
          CatalogCondition[],
        ],
    )
    .sort((a, b) => a[0].localeCompare(b[0]));
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/lib/catalog/schema.ts
================================================================================

import { z } from "zod";

export const ratingReferenceSchema = z.object({
  /** Rating schedule citation, e.g. "38 CFR 4.71a". */
  citation: z.string().min(1),
  note: z.string().min(1),
  /**
   * True only after a qualified human has confirmed the citation. Never set by
   * an automated agent.
   */
  verified: z.boolean(),
});

/**
 * One condition, identified once for the whole app. The catalog answers "does
 * this condition exist and what is it called". It deliberately says nothing
 * about how a condition connects to service: that is the knowledge base
 * (secondary) and the presumptive data (exposure).
 *
 * A rating schedule citation describes how VA evaluates severity. It is not
 * evidence of service connection and must never be presented as such.
 */
export const catalogConditionSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string().regex(/^[a-z0-9-]+$/),
  name: z.string().min(1),
  aliases: z.array(z.string().min(1)),
  bodySystem: z.string().min(1),
  /** May be empty when no citation was supplied. Never invent one. */
  ratingSchedule: z.array(ratingReferenceSchema),
  /** Open questions a reviewer must resolve. */
  reviewNotes: z.array(z.string().min(1)),
});

export const catalogFileSchema = z.object({
  bodySystem: z.string().min(1),
  conditions: z.array(catalogConditionSchema).min(1),
});

export const conditionCatalogSchema = z.array(catalogConditionSchema);

export type RatingReference = z.infer<typeof ratingReferenceSchema>;
export type CatalogCondition = z.infer<typeof catalogConditionSchema>;
export type ConditionCatalog = z.infer<typeof conditionCatalogSchema>;

FILE: src/lib/engine/evaluation.ts
================================================================================

import type { Recommendation } from "./types";

export interface EvidenceEvaluation {
  conditionId: string;
  satisfiedGaps: string[];
  remainingGaps: string[];
}

/**
 * Evaluates recommendations against uploaded evidence metadata indicators.
 * Provides checklist tracking without performing medical diagnostics or PII storage.
 */
export function evaluateEvidence(
  recommendations: Recommendation[],
  derivedIndicators: string[]
): EvidenceEvaluation[] {
  return recommendations.map((rec) => {
    const satisfied: string[] = [];
    const remaining: string[] = [];

    for (const gap of rec.evidenceGaps) {
      if (derivedIndicators.length > 0 && (gap.toLowerCase().includes("diagnosis") || gap.toLowerCase().includes("record"))) {
        satisfied.push(gap);
      } else {
        remaining.push(gap);
      }
    }

    return {
      conditionId: rec.conditionId,
      satisfiedGaps: satisfied,
      remainingGaps: remaining.length > 0 ? remaining : rec.evidenceGaps
    };
  });
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/lib/engine/export.ts
================================================================================

import type { Recommendation } from "./types";
import type { EvidenceEvaluation } from "./evaluation";

/**
 * Formats secondary claim research recommendations into a clean exportable text document
 * suitable for review with a VSO or medical representative.
 */
export function formatClaimSummary(
  recommendations: Recommendation[],
  evaluations: EvidenceEvaluation[]
): string {
  const lines: Array<string> = [
    "SECONDARY CLAIM RESEARCH & EVIDENCE CHECKLIST",
    "Generated by Secondary Condition Finder (38 CFR § 3.310 Research Tool)",
    "=".repeat(60),
    ""
  ];

  recommendations.forEach((rec, index) => {
    const evaluation = evaluations.find(e => e.conditionId === rec.conditionId);
    lines.push(`${index + 1}. Recommended Theory: ${rec.condition} (${rec.bodySystem})`);
    lines.push(`   Prevalence Category: ${rec.prevalence}`);
    lines.push(`   Contributing Primaries: ${rec.contributingPrimaries.map(p => p.name).join(", ")}`);
    
    if (evaluation) {
      lines.push(`   Satisfied Gaps: ${evaluation.satisfiedGaps.join("; ") || "None yet"}`);
      lines.push(`   Remaining Gaps: ${evaluation.remainingGaps.join("; ")}`);
    }

    lines.push("   Key Sources:");
    rec.sourceReferences.forEach(ref => {
      lines.push(`     - ${ref.citation}: ${ref.note}`);
    });
    lines.push("-".repeat(40));
  });

  lines.push("");
  lines.push("DISCLAIMER: This document is for educational and research preparation purposes only.");
  lines.push("It does not constitute medical diagnosis, legal advice, or a guaranteed VA claim outcome.");

  return lines.join("\n");
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/lib/engine/ingestion.ts
================================================================================

export interface SafeIngestionResult {
  success: boolean;
  fileCount: number;
  derivedIndicators: string[];
  error?: string;
}

/**
 * Safe, non-PII-retentive file ingestion adapter.
 * Extracts minimal derived metadata (e.g. file counts, format categories)
 * without storing raw file contents or personal identifiers.
 */
export function processEvidenceFiles(fileNames: string[]): SafeIngestionResult {
  try {
    if (!Array.isArray(fileNames)) {
      return { success: false, fileCount: 0, derivedIndicators: [], error: "Invalid file input format" };
    }

    // Extract minimal structural metadata only - NO content text is retained or logged
    const derivedIndicators = fileNames.map(name => {
      const ext = name.split(".").pop()?.toLowerCase() || "unknown";
      return `format-${ext}`;
    });

    return {
      success: true,
      fileCount: fileNames.length,
      derivedIndicators: [...new Set(derivedIndicators)]
    };
  } catch (err) {
    return { success: false, fileCount: 0, derivedIndicators: [], error: "Ingestion processing error" };
  }
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/lib/engine/recommend.ts
================================================================================

import type { ConditionCatalog } from "@/lib/catalog/schema";
import { findCondition, indexById, normalizeConditionName } from "@/lib/catalog/lookup";
import type { KnowledgeBase, SourceReference } from "@/lib/kb/schema";
import { publishedMappings } from "@/lib/kb/lookup";

import type {
  ContributingPrimary,
  Intake,
  Recommendation,
  RecommendationResult,
} from "./types";

export const ENGINE_VERSION = "phase-1";

const PREVALENCE_ORDER = {
  "commonly-explored": 0,
  "sometimes-explored": 1,
  "occasionally-explored": 2,
} as const;

const PREVALENCE_BY_RANK = [
  "commonly-explored",
  "sometimes-explored",
  "occasionally-explored",
] as const;

/**
 * Rank 1 contributes 1.0, rank 2 contributes 0.5, and so on. When several
 * primary conditions point at the same secondary, the contributions add, so a
 * suggestion supported by two primaries outranks one supported by a single
 * primary at the same rank. This is an ordering heuristic, nothing more, and
 * it is never shown to the user as a number.
 */
function rankWeight(rank: number): number {
  return 1 / rank;
}

function dedupe(values: string[]): string[] {
  return [...new Set(values)];
}

function dedupeReferences(references: SourceReference[]): SourceReference[] {
  const seen = new Map<string, SourceReference>();
  for (const reference of references) {
    if (!seen.has(reference.citation)) seen.set(reference.citation, reference);
  }
  return [...seen.values()];
}

/** Intake terms whose text overlaps the suggestion. Display only. */
function matchIntakeContext(condition: string, intake: Intake): string[] {
  const terms = [...intake.symptoms, ...intake.medications, ...intake.exposures];
  const target = normalizeConditionName(condition);

  return dedupe(
    terms.filter((term) => {
      const normalized = normalizeConditionName(term);
      if (normalized.length < 3) return false;
      return target.includes(normalized) || normalized.includes(target);
    }),
  );
}

interface Accumulated {
  conditionId: string;
  score: number;
  prevalenceRank: number;
  contributingPrimaries: ContributingPrimary[];
  evidenceGaps: string[];
  sourceReferences: SourceReference[];
}

export function recommend(
  catalog: ConditionCatalog,
  kb: KnowledgeBase,
  intake: Intake,
): RecommendationResult {
  const byId = indexById(catalog);
  const mappings = publishedMappings(kb);

  const recognizedPrimaries: string[] = [];
  const unmappedPrimaries: string[] = [];
  const unrecognizedPrimaries: string[] = [];
  const recognizedIds = new Set<string>();

  for (const input of dedupe(intake.primaryConditions.map((c) => c.trim()))) {
    const condition = findCondition(catalog, input);

    if (!condition) {
      unrecognizedPrimaries.push(input);
      continue;
    }
    if (recognizedIds.has(condition.id) || unmappedPrimaries.includes(condition.name)) {
      continue;
    }
    if (!mappings.has(condition.id)) {
      unmappedPrimaries.push(condition.name);
      continue;
    }

    recognizedIds.add(condition.id);
    recognizedPrimaries.push(condition.name);
  }

  const accumulator = new Map<string, Accumulated>();

  for (const primaryId of recognizedIds) {
    const mapping = mappings.get(primaryId)!;
    const primary = byId.get(primaryId)!;

    for (const suggestion of mapping.suggestions) {
      // Do not suggest something the user already listed as service connected.
      if (recognizedIds.has(suggestion.conditionId)) continue;

      const existing = accumulator.get(suggestion.conditionId);
      const contribution: ContributingPrimary = {
        id: primary.id,
        name: primary.name,
        rank: suggestion.rank,
        rationale: suggestion.rationale,
      };

      if (existing) {
        existing.score += rankWeight(suggestion.rank);
        existing.contributingPrimaries.push(contribution);
        existing.evidenceGaps = dedupe([
          ...existing.evidenceGaps,
          ...suggestion.evidenceGaps,
        ]);
        existing.sourceReferences = dedupeReferences([
          ...existing.sourceReferences,
          ...suggestion.sourceReferences,
        ]);
        existing.prevalenceRank = Math.min(
          existing.prevalenceRank,
          PREVALENCE_ORDER[suggestion.prevalence],
        );
      } else {
        accumulator.set(suggestion.conditionId, {
          conditionId: suggestion.conditionId,
          score: rankWeight(suggestion.rank),
          prevalenceRank: PREVALENCE_ORDER[suggestion.prevalence],
          contributingPrimaries: [contribution],
          evidenceGaps: [...suggestion.evidenceGaps],
          sourceReferences: [...suggestion.sourceReferences],
        });
      }
    }
  }

  let recommendations: Recommendation[] = [...accumulator.values()]
    .map((entry) => {
      const catalogEntry = byId.get(entry.conditionId);
      if (!catalogEntry) {
        // Referential integrity is enforced by test; this guards runtime.
        throw new Error(
          `Suggestion references unknown catalog condition "${entry.conditionId}".`,
        );
      }
      return {
        conditionId: entry.conditionId,
        condition: catalogEntry.name,
        bodySystem: catalogEntry.bodySystem,
        prevalence: PREVALENCE_BY_RANK[entry.prevalenceRank],
        score: Number(entry.score.toFixed(4)),
        contributingPrimaries: [...entry.contributingPrimaries].sort(
          (a, b) => a.rank - b.rank || a.name.localeCompare(b.name),
        ),
        evidenceGaps: entry.evidenceGaps,
        sourceReferences: entry.sourceReferences,
        matchedIntakeContext: matchIntakeContext(catalogEntry.name, intake),
      };
    })
    .sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score || a.condition.localeCompare(b.condition));

  // Focus Engine: Constrain recommendations strictly to user-selected diagnosed conditions
  if (intake.diagnosedConditions && intake.diagnosedConditions.length > 0) {
    const diagnosedSet = new Set(
      intake.diagnosedConditions.map((c: string) => normalizeConditionName(c))
    );
    recommendations = recommendations.filter(rec => 
      diagnosedSet.has(normalizeConditionName(rec.condition))
    );
  }

  return {
    recommendations,
    recognizedPrimaries,
    unmappedPrimaries,
    unrecognizedPrimaries,
  };
}

/**
 * Scenario to Wizard Adapter
 * Bridges legacy scenario triggers into the 6-step secondary wizard UX.
 */

export interface ScenarioPayload {
  scenarioId: string;
  primaryCondition: string;
  secondaryTarget: string;
  evidenceNotes: string[];
}

export const DEMO_SCENARIOS: Record<string, ScenarioPayload> = {
  "ptsd-sleep-apnea": {
    scenarioId: "ptsd-sleep-apnea",
    primaryCondition: "ptsd",
    secondaryTarget: "sleep-apnea",
    evidenceNotes: ["Polysomnography report attached", "Treatment logs showing chronic sleep disruption"]
  },
  "lumbar-radiculopathy": {
    scenarioId: "lumbar-radiculopathy",
    primaryCondition: "lumbar-strain",
    secondaryTarget: "radiculopathy",
    evidenceNotes: ["EMG nerve conduction study", "Lower extremity pain radiation documentation"]
  }
};

export function getScenarioWizardRoute(scenarioId: string): string {
  const scenario = DEMO_SCENARIOS[scenarioId];
  if (!scenario) return "/secondary/upload";
  
  // Route scenario directly into wizard suggestions/evidence review with prefilled context
  return `/secondary/suggestions?scenario=${scenarioId}&primary=${scenario.primaryCondition}&target=${scenario.secondaryTarget}`;
}

import { z } from "zod";

export const intakeSchema = z.object({
  diagnosedConditions: z.array(z.string()).optional(),
  primaryConditions: z.array(z.string().min(1)).min(1),
  symptoms: z.array(z.string()).default([]),
  medications: z.array(z.string()).default([]),
  exposures: z.array(z.string()).default([]),
  notes: z.string().default(""),

export type Intake = z.infer<typeof intakeSchema>;

export interface ContributingPrimary {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  rank: number;
  rationale: string;

export interface Recommendation {
  conditionId: string;
  condition: string;
  bodySystem: string;
  prevalence: "commonly-explored" | "sometimes-explored" | "occasionally-explored";
  score: number;
  contributingPrimaries: ContributingPrimary[];
  evidenceGaps: string[];
  sourceReferences: Array<{ citation: string; note: string; verified: boolean }>;
  matchedIntakeContext: string[];

================================================================================
FILE: src/lib/events/privacy-guard.ts
================================================================================

/**
 * Event Logging Privacy & Schema Evolution Guard
 * Ensures zero persistence of intake free text, clinical notes, or PII.
 */

export const PROHIBITED_LOG_FIELDS = [
  "freeText",
  "rawNotes",
  "patientName",
  "ssn",
  "diagnosisNotes",
  "medicalHistoryText",
  "userInputValue"
];

export interface EventPayload {
  eventType: string;
  timestamp: string;
  metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;
  [key: string]: unknown;
}

export function sanitizeAndValidateEventPayload(payload: EventPayload): {
  isValid: boolean;
  sanitizedPayload: EventPayload;
  violations: string[];
} {
  const violations: string[] = [];
  const sanitizedPayload: EventPayload = { ...payload };

  PROHIBITED_LOG_FIELDS.forEach(field => {
    if (field in sanitizedPayload) {
      violations.push(field);
      delete sanitizedPayload[field];
    }
    if (sanitizedPayload.metadata && typeof sanitizedPayload.metadata === 'object') {
      if (field in sanitizedPayload.metadata) {
        violations.push(`metadata.${field}`);
        delete (sanitizedPayload.metadata as Record<string, unknown>)[field];
      }
    }
  });

  return {
    isValid: violations.length === 0,
    sanitizedPayload,
    violations
  };
}

FILE: src/lib/guardrails.ts
