/**
 * Centralized Legal and Medical Guardrails
 * Enforces compliance across /, /secondary, /presumptive, and /results surfaces.
 */

export const MEDICAL_DISCLAIMER = "Educational guidance tool only. This tool does not provide medical diagnoses, legal advice, or official VA claim adjudication.";
export const NEVER_DIAGNOSTIC_NOTICE = "Informational estimate only — this platform is never diagnostic and does not establish a medical condition.";
export const UNVERIFIED_CITATION_WARNING = "Regulatory reference requires professional verification or independent medical validation.";

export const FORBIDDEN_PHRASES = [
  "you have been diagnosed with",
  "guaranteed VA rating",
  "official medical diagnosis",
  "this replaces a C&P exam",
  "approved claim"
];

export function validateGuardrails(text: string): { isValid: boolean; violations: string[] } {
  const lower = text.toLowerCase();
  const violations = FORBIDDEN_PHRASES.filter(phrase => lower.includes(phrase));
  return {
    isValid: violations.length === 0,
    violations
  };
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/lib/kb/load.ts
================================================================================

import "server-only";

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

import { loadConditionCatalog } from "@/lib/catalog/load";

import {
  conditionMappingSchema,
  knowledgeBaseSchema,
  type ConditionMapping,
  type KnowledgeBase,
} from "./schema";
import { validateKnowledgeBaseAgainstCatalog } from "./validate";

const KB_DIR = path.join(process.cwd(), "data", "knowledge-base");

let cached: KnowledgeBase | null = null;

/**
 * Reads and validates every mapping file.
 *
 * Validation failures throw rather than degrade. A malformed mapping in a
 * claims tool is a correctness problem, not something to silently skip.
 */
export function loadKnowledgeBase(): KnowledgeBase {
  if (cached) return cached;

  const files = fs
    .readdirSync(KB_DIR)
    .filter((file) => file.endsWith(".json"))
    .sort();

  const mappings: ConditionMapping[] = files.map((file) => {
    const raw = fs.readFileSync(path.join(KB_DIR, file), "utf8");
    const parsed = conditionMappingSchema.safeParse(JSON.parse(raw));

    if (!parsed.success) {
      throw new Error(`Invalid mapping in ${file}: ${parsed.error.message}`);
    }

    const expectedId = path.basename(file, ".json");
    if (parsed.data.id !== expectedId) {
      throw new Error(
        `Mapping id mismatch in ${file}: id is "${parsed.data.id}", expected "${expectedId}".`,
      );
    }

    return parsed.data;
  });

  const parsed = knowledgeBaseSchema.parse(mappings);
  validateKnowledgeBaseAgainstCatalog(loadConditionCatalog(), parsed);
  cached = parsed;
  return cached;
}

/** Test seam: clears the module-level cache. */
export function resetKnowledgeBaseCache(): void {
  cached = null;
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/lib/kb/lookup.ts
================================================================================

import type { KnowledgeBase, ConditionMapping } from "./schema";

/** Published mappings, keyed by catalog id. Excludes drafts. */
export function publishedMappings(
  kb: KnowledgeBase,
): Map<string, ConditionMapping> {
  return new Map(
    kb.filter((mapping) => !mapping.draft).map((mapping) => [mapping.id, mapping]),
  );
}

/** Ids that have published mappings, so intake can show which are covered. */
export function mappedConditionIds(kb: KnowledgeBase): Set<string> {
  return new Set(
    kb.filter((mapping) => !mapping.draft).map((mapping) => mapping.id),
  );
}

/** Draft mappings awaiting human-authored content. For tooling and review. */
export function draftMappings(kb: KnowledgeBase): ConditionMapping[] {
  return kb.filter((mapping) => mapping.draft);
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/lib/kb/schema.ts
================================================================================

import { z } from "zod";

/**
 * Prevalence is deliberately qualitative. We do not emit numeric probabilities:
 * the knowledge base is curated, not statistically derived, and a percentage
 * would imply a precision we cannot support in a claims context.
 */
export const prevalenceSchema = z.enum([
  "commonly-explored",
  "sometimes-explored",
  "occasionally-explored",
]);

export const sourceReferenceSchema = z.object({
  /** Legal or procedural citation, e.g. "38 CFR 3.310". */
  citation: z.string().trim().min(1),
  /** Plain-language explanation of what the citation covers. */
  note: z.string().trim().min(1),
  /**
   * True only after a qualified human has confirmed the citation is accurate
   * and applies to this suggestion. Never set to true by an automated agent.
   */
  verified: z.literal(false),
});

export const suggestionSchema = z.object({
  /**
   * Catalog id of the secondary condition. Display names are resolved from the
   * catalog, so a label can never drift between two mapping files.
   */
  conditionId: z.string().regex(/^[a-z0-9-]+$/),
  rank: z.number().int().positive(),
  prevalence: prevalenceSchema,
  rationale: z.string().min(1),
  /** What a claim on this theory typically still needs. At least one. */
  evidenceGaps: z.array(z.string().min(1)).min(1),
  sourceReferences: z.array(sourceReferenceSchema).min(1).max(3),
});

/**
 * Secondary-condition mappings for one primary. Identity (name, aliases, body
 * system, rating citations) lives in the catalog; this file holds only the
 * edges out of that condition.
 */
export const conditionMappingSchema = z
  .object({
    /** Catalog id. Must match the filename. */
    id: z.string().regex(/^[a-z0-9-]+$/),
    /**
     * Draft mappings are validated and version controlled but never surfaced.
     * Use while authoring is in progress.
     */
    draft: z.boolean().default(false),
    /** Checklist of what a reviewer must supply. Only meaningful on drafts. */
    todo: z.array(z.string().min(1)).default([]),
    suggestions: z.array(suggestionSchema).default([]),
  })
  .superRefine((mapping, ctx) => {
    if (!mapping.draft && mapping.suggestions.length === 0) {
      ctx.addIssue({
        code: "custom",
        path: ["suggestions"],
        message:
          "A published mapping needs at least one suggestion. Mark it draft: true while it is incomplete.",
      });
    }
    if (!mapping.draft && mapping.todo.length > 0) {
      ctx.addIssue({
        code: "custom",
        path: ["todo"],
        message:
          "Resolve every todo before publishing, or keep the mapping draft: true.",
      });
    }
    if (mapping.draft && mapping.todo.length === 0) {
      ctx.addIssue({
        code: "custom",
        path: ["todo"],
        message: "A draft must state what is still needed.",
      });
    }
    const ranks = mapping.suggestions.map((s) => s.rank).sort((a, b) => a - b);
    if (ranks.some((rank, index) => rank !== index + 1)) {
      ctx.addIssue({
        code: "custom",
        path: ["suggestions"],
        message: "Ranks must be unique and gapless, starting at 1.",
      });
    }
    if (mapping.suggestions.some((s) => s.conditionId === mapping.id)) {
      ctx.addIssue({
        code: "custom",
        path: ["suggestions"],
        message: "A condition cannot be a secondary of itself.",
      });
    }
  });

export const knowledgeBaseSchema = z.array(conditionMappingSchema);

export type Prevalence = z.infer<typeof prevalenceSchema>;
export type SourceReference = z.infer<typeof sourceReferenceSchema>;
export type Suggestion = z.infer<typeof suggestionSchema>;
export type ConditionMapping = z.infer<typeof conditionMappingSchema>;
export type KnowledgeBase = z.infer<typeof knowledgeBaseSchema>;


================================================================================
FILE: src/lib/kb/validate.ts
================================================================================

import { indexById } from "@/lib/catalog/lookup";
import type { ConditionCatalog } from "@/lib/catalog/schema";

import type { KnowledgeBase } from "./schema";

/**
 * Fails fast when mappings drift from the catalog.
 *
 * Tests enforce the same invariant, but this guards every runtime path that
 * loads the knowledge base.
 */
export function validateKnowledgeBaseAgainstCatalog(
  catalog: ConditionCatalog,
  kb: KnowledgeBase,
): void {
  const byId = indexById(catalog);

  for (const mapping of kb) {
    if (!byId.has(mapping.id)) {
      throw new Error(
        `Mapping "${mapping.id}" has no matching condition in data/conditions.`,
      );
    }

    for (const suggestion of mapping.suggestions) {
      if (!byId.has(suggestion.conditionId)) {
        throw new Error(
          `Mapping "${mapping.id}" suggests unknown condition "${suggestion.conditionId}".`,
        );
      }
    }
  }
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/lib/log/index.ts
================================================================================

import "server-only";

import path from "node:path";

import { createMemoryLogger } from "./memory";
import type { EventLogger } from "./types";

let logger: EventLogger | null = null;

/**
 * SQLite when a writable path is configured (local dev, self-hosted),
 * otherwise an in-process logger that emits structured stdout lines.
 * Phase 2 swaps the SQLite implementation for Postgres without touching
 * any caller.
 *
 * Async so the native SQLite module is only loaded when it is actually
 * configured. Serverless bundles that never set the env var never touch it.
 */
export async function getEventLogger(): Promise<EventLogger> {
  if (logger) return logger;

  const dbPath = process.env.EVENT_LOG_SQLITE_PATH;

  if (dbPath) {
    const { createSqliteLogger } = await import("./sqlite");
    logger = createSqliteLogger(path.resolve(dbPath));
  } else {
    logger = createMemoryLogger();
  }

  return logger;
}

export type { EventLogger, RecommendationEvent } from "./types";


================================================================================
FILE: src/lib/log/memory.ts
================================================================================

import type { EventLogger, RecommendationEvent } from "./types";

/**
 * In-process logger used by tests and by deployments with ephemeral disks.
 * Also writes a single structured line to stdout so events remain traceable
 * through the platform's log drain.
 */
export function createMemoryLogger(options?: { silent?: boolean }): EventLogger {
  const events: RecommendationEvent[] = [];

  return {
    record(event) {
      events.unshift(event);
      if (!options?.silent) {
        console.log(JSON.stringify({ type: "recommendation", ...event }));
      }
    },
    recent(limit) {
      return events.slice(0, limit);
    },
  };
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/lib/log/sqlite.ts
================================================================================

import "server-only";

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

import Database from "better-sqlite3";

import type { EventLogger } from "./types";

/**
 * Local, file-backed logger for development and self-hosted deployments.
 * Not suitable for serverless targets with ephemeral disks; see index.ts for
 * how the logger is selected at runtime.
 */
export function createSqliteLogger(filePath: string): EventLogger {
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(filePath), { recursive: true });
  const db = new Database(filePath);

  db.pragma("journal_mode = WAL");
  db.exec(`
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recommendation_events (
      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
      created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
      recognized_primaries TEXT NOT NULL,
      unrecognized_primary_count INTEGER NOT NULL,
      recommended_conditions TEXT NOT NULL,
      engine_version TEXT NOT NULL
    );
  `);

  const insert = db.prepare(`
    INSERT INTO recommendation_events
      (id, created_at, recognized_primaries, unrecognized_primary_count,
       recommended_conditions, engine_version)
    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
  `);

  const select = db.prepare(`
    SELECT * FROM recommendation_events ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT ?
  `);

  return {
    record(event) {
      insert.run(
        event.id,
        event.createdAt,
        JSON.stringify(event.recognizedPrimaries),
        event.unrecognizedPrimaryCount,
        JSON.stringify(event.recommendedConditions),
        event.engineVersion,
      );
    },
    recent(limit) {
      const rows = select.all(limit) as Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
      return rows.map((row) => ({
        id: String(row.id),
        createdAt: String(row.created_at),
        recognizedPrimaries: JSON.parse(String(row.recognized_primaries)),
        unrecognizedPrimaryCount: Number(row.unrecognized_primary_count),
        recommendedConditions: JSON.parse(String(row.recommended_conditions)),
        engineVersion: String(row.engine_version),
      }));
    },
  };
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/lib/log/types.ts
================================================================================

/**
 * Traceability record for a single recommendation run.
 *
 * Phase 1 stores no personally identifying information. Intake free text is
 * NOT persisted: only the resolved condition names and counts, which is
 * enough to explain why a given set of suggestions was produced.
 */
export interface RecommendationEvent {
  id: string;
  createdAt: string;
  recognizedPrimaries: string[];
  unrecognizedPrimaryCount: number;
  recommendedConditions: string[];
  engineVersion: string;
}

export interface EventLogger {
  record(event: RecommendationEvent): void;
  recent(limit: number): RecommendationEvent[];
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/lib/presumptive/load.ts
================================================================================

import "server-only";

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

import {
  exposureCatalogSchema,
  exposureCategorySchema,
  type ExposureCatalog,
  type ExposureCategory,
} from "./schema";

const PRESUMPTIVES_DIR = path.join(process.cwd(), "data", "presumptives");

let cached: ExposureCatalog | null = null;

/**
 * Reads and validates every exposure category. Validation failures throw
 * rather than degrade: a malformed date window would silently change who the
 * app tells a veteran they might be eligible under.
 */
export function loadExposureCatalog(): ExposureCatalog {
  if (cached) return cached;

  const files = fs
    .readdirSync(PRESUMPTIVES_DIR)
    .filter((file) => file.endsWith(".json"))
    .sort();

  const categories: ExposureCategory[] = files.map((file) => {
    const raw = fs.readFileSync(path.join(PRESUMPTIVES_DIR, file), "utf8");
    const parsed = exposureCategorySchema.safeParse(JSON.parse(raw));

    if (!parsed.success) {
      throw new Error(
        `Invalid exposure category in ${file}: ${parsed.error.message}`,
      );
    }

    const expectedId = path.basename(file, ".json");
    if (parsed.data.id !== expectedId) {
      throw new Error(
        `Exposure category id mismatch in ${file}: id is "${parsed.data.id}", expected "${expectedId}".`,
      );
    }

    return parsed.data;
  });

  cached = exposureCatalogSchema.parse(categories);
  return cached;
}

/** Test seam: clears the module-level cache. */
export function resetExposureCatalogCache(): void {
  cached = null;
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/lib/presumptive/match.ts
================================================================================

import { z } from "zod";

import type {
  ExposureCatalog,
  ExposureCategory,
  PresumptiveCondition,
  QualifyingService,
} from "./schema";

export const serviceHistorySchema = z
  .object({
    /** Location labels the user selected, matched exactly against the data. */
    locations: z.array(z.string().min(1)).min(1, {
      message: "Select at least one location where you served.",
    }),
    serviceStart: z.string().regex(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/).nullable().default(null),
    serviceEnd: z.string().regex(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/).nullable().default(null),
  })
  .refine(
    (value) =>
      !value.serviceStart ||
      !value.serviceEnd ||
      value.serviceStart <= value.serviceEnd,
    { message: "Service start date must be on or before the end date.", path: ["serviceEnd"] },
  );

export type ServiceHistory = z.infer<typeof serviceHistorySchema>;

export interface MatchedPeriod {
  period: QualifyingService;
  matchedLocations: string[];
  /**
   * False when the user supplied no dates. The location qualifies, but whether
   * the date window is met is unconfirmed and must be said out loud.
   */
  datesConfirmed: boolean;
}

export interface CategoryMatch {
  category: ExposureCategory;
  matchedPeriods: MatchedPeriod[];
  conditions: PresumptiveCondition[];
}

export interface PresumptiveResult {
  matches: CategoryMatch[];
  /** Locations the user selected that matched no qualifying period. */
  unmatchedLocations: string[];
  /**
   * True when at least one match rests on location alone because no service
   * dates were supplied.
   */
  hasUnconfirmedDates: boolean;
}

/** ISO date strings compare correctly with string comparison. */
function rangesOverlap(
  aStart: string,
  aEnd: string | null,
  bStart: string,
  bEnd: string | null,
): boolean {
  if (aEnd && aEnd < bStart) return false;
  if (bEnd && bEnd < aStart) return false;
  return true;
}

/** Every distinct location across the catalog, for building the UI. */
export function listLocations(catalog: ExposureCatalog): string[] {
  const locations = new Set<string>();
  for (const category of catalog) {
    for (const period of category.qualifyingService) {
      for (const location of period.locations) locations.add(location);
    }
  }
  return [...locations].sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b));
}

/**
 * Matches a service history against exposure categories.
 *
 * This reports which presumptions *may* apply. It is not an eligibility
 * determination: VA also considers discharge characterization, the diagnosis
 * itself, and evidence that can rebut a presumption under 38 CFR 3.307(d).
 */
export function matchPresumptives(
  catalog: ExposureCatalog,
  history: ServiceHistory,
): PresumptiveResult {
  const selected = [...new Set(history.locations)];
  const matchedLocationSet = new Set<string>();
  const matches: CategoryMatch[] = [];
  let hasUnconfirmedDates = false;

  for (const category of catalog) {
    const matchedPeriods: MatchedPeriod[] = [];

    for (const period of category.qualifyingService) {
      const matchedLocations = selected.filter((location) =>
        period.locations.includes(location),
      );
      if (matchedLocations.length === 0) continue;

      const datesConfirmed = Boolean(history.serviceStart || history.serviceEnd);

      if (datesConfirmed) {
        const start = history.serviceStart ?? history.serviceEnd!;
        const end = history.serviceEnd ?? history.serviceStart!;
        if (!rangesOverlap(start, end, period.startDate, period.endDate)) {
          continue;
        }
      } else {
        hasUnconfirmedDates = true;
      }

      for (const location of matchedLocations) matchedLocationSet.add(location);
      matchedPeriods.push({ period, matchedLocations, datesConfirmed });
    }

    if (matchedPeriods.length > 0) {
      matches.push({
        category,
        matchedPeriods,
        conditions: [...category.conditions].sort(
          (a, b) => a.group.localeCompare(b.group) || a.name.localeCompare(b.name),
        ),
      });
    }
  }

  return {
    matches,
    unmatchedLocations: selected.filter(
      (location) => !matchedLocationSet.has(location),
    ),
    hasUnconfirmedDates,
  };
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/lib/presumptive/schema.ts
================================================================================

import { z } from "zod";

const isoDate = z.string().regex(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/, {
  message: "Dates must be ISO yyyy-mm-dd.",
});

export const presumptiveSourceSchema = z.object({
  citation: z.string().min(1),
  note: z.string().min(1),
  /** Public URL a reviewer can open to check the citation. */
  url: z.string().url(),
  /**
   * True only after a qualified human has confirmed the citation. Never set by
   * an automated agent, same rule as the secondary-condition knowledge base.
   */
  verified: z.literal(false),
});

/**
 * A location and date window that establishes presumed exposure. Presumptive
 * service connection turns on where and when someone served, not on any other
 * service-connected condition.
 */
export const qualifyingServiceSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string().regex(/^[a-z0-9-]+$/),
  label: z.string().min(1),
  locations: z.array(z.string().min(1)).min(1),
  startDate: isoDate,
  /** Null means the window is still open. */
  endDate: isoDate.nullable(),
  note: z.string().nullable(),
});

export const presumptiveConditionSchema = z.object({
  conditionId: z.string().regex(/^[a-z0-9-]+$/),
  name: z.string().min(1),
  /** Grouping for display, e.g. "Cancers" or "Respiratory conditions". */
  group: z.string().min(1),
  /**
   * Conditions carrying an extra requirement beyond diagnosis, such as a
   * manifestation deadline or a timing rule. Null when none applies.
   */
  qualifier: z.string().nullable(),
});

export const exposureCategorySchema = z.object({
  id: z.string().regex(/^[a-z0-9-]+$/),
  name: z.string().min(1),
  authority: z.string().min(1),
  summary: z.string().min(1),
  qualifyingService: z.array(qualifyingServiceSchema).min(1),
  conditions: z.array(presumptiveConditionSchema).min(1),
  sourceReferences: z.array(presumptiveSourceSchema).min(1),
  /** Date the list was last checked against the VA source. */
  sourceCheckedOn: isoDate,
});

export const exposureCatalogSchema = z.array(exposureCategorySchema);

export type PresumptiveSource = z.infer<typeof presumptiveSourceSchema>;
export type QualifyingService = z.infer<typeof qualifyingServiceSchema>;
export type PresumptiveCondition = z.infer<typeof presumptiveConditionSchema>;
export type ExposureCategory = z.infer<typeof exposureCategorySchema>;
export type ExposureCatalog = z.infer<typeof exposureCatalogSchema>;

================================================================================
FILE: src/lib/secondary-workflow/state.ts
================================================================================

import { z } from "zod";

import { indexById } from "@/lib/catalog/lookup";
import type { ConditionCatalog } from "@/lib/catalog/schema";

export interface SecondaryWorkflowState {
  diagnosedConditionIds: string[];
  serviceConnectedConditionIds: string[];
  ratingsByConditionId: Record<string, number | null>;
  documentsShared: boolean;
}

export type QueryValue = string | string[] | undefined;
export type QueryParams = Record<string, QueryValue>;

const diagnosedSelectionSchema = z.object({
  diagnosedConditionIds: z.array(z.string().min(1)).min(1, {
    message: "Select at least one diagnosed condition to continue.",
  }),
});

const serviceConnectedSelectionSchema = z
  .object({
    diagnosedConditionIds: z.array(z.string().min(1)).min(1),
    serviceConnectedConditionIds: z.array(z.string().min(1)).min(1, {
      message: "Select at least one condition that is already service connected.",
    }),
  })
  .refine(
    (value) =>
      value.serviceConnectedConditionIds.every((id) =>
        value.diagnosedConditionIds.includes(id),
      ),
    {
      message:
        "Service-connected selections must come from the diagnosed conditions you selected.",
      path: ["serviceConnectedConditionIds"],
    },
  );

const documentsSharedSchema = z.object({
  documentsShared: z.enum(["yes", "no"]),
});

function toArray(value: QueryValue): string[] {
  if (!value) return [];

  return (Array.isArray(value) ? value : [value])
    .flatMap((entry) => entry.split(","))
    .map((entry) => entry.trim())
    .filter(Boolean);
}

function first(value: QueryValue): string | undefined {
  if (!value) return undefined;
  return Array.isArray(value) ? value[0] : value;
}

function dedupe(values: string[]): string[] {
  return [...new Set(values)];
}

function toKnownConditionIds(catalog: ConditionCatalog, ids: string[]): string[] {
  const known = indexById(catalog);
  return dedupe(ids).filter((id) => known.has(id));
}

function parseRawDiagnosedConditionIds(
  params: QueryParams,
  catalog: ConditionCatalog,
): string[] {
  return toKnownConditionIds(catalog, toArray(params.diagnosed));
}

function parseRawServiceConnectedConditionIds(
  params: QueryParams,
  diagnosedConditionIds: string[],
): string[] {
  const explicit = toArray(params.serviceConnected);
  if (explicit.length > 0) {
    return dedupe(explicit).filter((id) => diagnosedConditionIds.includes(id));
  }

  return diagnosedConditionIds.filter(
    (id) => first(params[`serviceConnected-${id}`]) === "yes",
  );
}

export function parseDiagnosedPrefill(
  params: QueryParams,
  catalog: ConditionCatalog,
): string[] {
  return parseRawDiagnosedConditionIds(params, catalog);
}

export function parseServiceConnectedPrefill(
  params: QueryParams,
  catalog: ConditionCatalog,
): { diagnosedConditionIds: string[]; serviceConnectedConditionIds: string[] } {
  const diagnosedConditionIds = parseDiagnosedPrefill(params, catalog);

  return {
    diagnosedConditionIds,
    serviceConnectedConditionIds: parseRawServiceConnectedConditionIds(
      params,
      diagnosedConditionIds,
    ),
  };
}

export function parseDiagnosedSelection(
  params: QueryParams,
  catalog: ConditionCatalog,
): { diagnosedConditionIds: string[] } {
  return diagnosedSelectionSchema.parse({
    diagnosedConditionIds: parseRawDiagnosedConditionIds(params, catalog),
  });
}

export function parseServiceConnectedSelection(
  params: QueryParams,
  catalog: ConditionCatalog,
): { diagnosedConditionIds: string[]; serviceConnectedConditionIds: string[] } {
  const diagnosed = parseDiagnosedSelection(params, catalog).diagnosedConditionIds;

  return serviceConnectedSelectionSchema.parse({
    diagnosedConditionIds: diagnosed,
    serviceConnectedConditionIds: parseRawServiceConnectedConditionIds(
      params,
      diagnosed,
    ),
  });
}

export function ratingFieldName(conditionId: string): string {
  return `rating-${conditionId}`;
}

function parseRatingValue(raw: string | undefined): number | null {
  if (!raw || raw === "decline") return null;

  const parsed = Number(raw);
  if (!Number.isInteger(parsed) || parsed < 0 || parsed > 100) {
    throw new Error("Ratings must be whole numbers from 0 through 100.");
  }

  return parsed;
}

export function parseRatingsSelection(
  params: QueryParams,
  catalog: ConditionCatalog,
): {
  diagnosedConditionIds: string[];
  serviceConnectedConditionIds: string[];
  ratingsByConditionId: Record<string, number | null>;
} {
  const serviceConnected = parseServiceConnectedSelection(params, catalog);
  const ratingsByConditionId: Record<string, number | null> = {};

  for (const id of serviceConnected.serviceConnectedConditionIds) {
    ratingsByConditionId[id] = parseRatingValue(first(params[ratingFieldName(id)]));
  }

  return {
    diagnosedConditionIds: serviceConnected.diagnosedConditionIds,
    serviceConnectedConditionIds: serviceConnected.serviceConnectedConditionIds,
    ratingsByConditionId,
  };
}

export function parseRatingsPrefill(
  params: QueryParams,
  conditionIds: string[],
): Record<string, number | null> {
  const ratingsByConditionId: Record<string, number | null> = {};

  for (const id of conditionIds) {
    try {
      ratingsByConditionId[id] = parseRatingValue(first(params[ratingFieldName(id)]));
    } catch {
      ratingsByConditionId[id] = null;
    }
  }

  return ratingsByConditionId;
}

function parseDocumentsShared(value: QueryValue): boolean {
  const parsed = documentsSharedSchema.parse({
    documentsShared: first(value),
  });

  return parsed.documentsShared === "yes";
}

export function parseDocumentsSharedPrefill(value: QueryValue): boolean {
  return first(value) === "yes";
}

export function parseDocumentsSelection(
  params: QueryParams,
  catalog: ConditionCatalog,
): {
  diagnosedConditionIds: string[];
  serviceConnectedConditionIds: string[];
  ratingsByConditionId: Record<string, number | null>;
  documentsShared: boolean;
} {
  const ratings = parseRatingsSelection(params, catalog);

  return {
    ...ratings,
    documentsShared: parseDocumentsShared(params.documentsShared),
  };
}

export function parseWorkflowState(
  params: QueryParams,
  catalog: ConditionCatalog,
): SecondaryWorkflowState {
  return parseDocumentsSelection(params, catalog);
}

export function workflowStateToSearchParams(
  state: SecondaryWorkflowState,
): URLSearchParams {
  const searchParams = new URLSearchParams();

  for (const id of state.diagnosedConditionIds) {
    searchParams.append("diagnosed", id);
  }

  for (const id of state.serviceConnectedConditionIds) {
    searchParams.append("serviceConnected", id);
  }

  for (const id of state.serviceConnectedConditionIds) {
    const value = state.ratingsByConditionId[id];
    searchParams.set(ratingFieldName(id), value === null ? "decline" : String(value));
  }

  searchParams.set("documentsShared", state.documentsShared ? "yes" : "no");

  return searchParams;
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/lib/secondary-workflow/suggestions.ts
================================================================================

import { indexById } from "@/lib/catalog/lookup";
import type { ConditionCatalog } from "@/lib/catalog/schema";
import type { Recommendation } from "@/lib/engine/types";

export interface GroupedSecondarySuggestions {
  primaryId: string;
  primaryName: string;
  recommendations: Recommendation[];
}

export function groupSuggestionsByPrimary(
  catalog: ConditionCatalog,
  serviceConnectedConditionIds: string[],
  recommendations: Recommendation[],
): GroupedSecondarySuggestions[] {
  const byId = indexById(catalog);

  return serviceConnectedConditionIds
    .map((primaryId) => {
      const primary = byId.get(primaryId);
      if (!primary) return null;

      const scopedRecommendations = recommendations.filter((recommendation) =>
        recommendation.contributingPrimaries.some(
          (contributingPrimary) => contributingPrimary.id === primaryId,
        ),
      );

      return {
        primaryId,
        primaryName: primary.name,
        recommendations: scopedRecommendations,
      };
    })
    .filter((group): group is GroupedSecondarySuggestions => group !== null)
    .sort((a, b) => a.primaryName.localeCompare(b.primaryName));
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/lib/va/index.ts
================================================================================

export interface VaDisabilityRating {
  ratingPercentage: number;
  effectiveDate: string;
  conditionsConnected: { name: string; diagnosticCode: string; serviceConnected: boolean }[];
}

export interface VaAdapterConfig {
  enabled: boolean;
  timeoutMs?: number;
  mockFailureMode?: "none" | "timeout" | "error";
  baseUrl?: string;
  apiKey?: string;
}

export interface VaAdapterResult<T> {
  success: boolean;
  data: T | null;
  source: "va-sandbox-api" | "local-fallback";
  error?: string;
}

export async function fetchSandboxVABits(
  userId: string,
  config: VaAdapterConfig = { enabled: true, timeoutMs: 1000, mockFailureMode: "none" },
): Promise<VaAdapterResult<VaDisabilityRating>> {
  if (!config.enabled) {
    return {
      success: false,
      data: null,
      source: "local-fallback",
      error: "VA API integration is disabled by feature flag.",
    };
  }

  const timeout = config.timeoutMs || 1000;

  if (config.mockFailureMode === "timeout") {
    try {
      await new Promise((_, reject) =>
        setTimeout(() => reject(new Error("Gateway Timeout")), timeout),
      );
    } catch {
      return {
        success: false,
        data: null,
        source: "local-fallback",
        error: "Gateway Timeout: Upstream VA API did not respond within threshold.",
      };
    }
  }

  if (config.mockFailureMode === "error") {
    return {
      success: false,
      data: null,
      source: "local-fallback",
      error: "Internal Server Error (500): VA API backend failure.",
    };
  }

  return {
    success: true,
    data: {
      ratingPercentage: 70,
      effectiveDate: "2024-01-01",
      conditionsConnected: [
        {
          name: "Post-Traumatic Stress Disorder (PTSD)",
          diagnosticCode: "9411",
          serviceConnected: true,
        },
        { name: "Tinnitus", diagnosticCode: "6260", serviceConnected: true },
      ],
    },
    source: "va-sandbox-api",
  };
}

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { renderToStaticMarkup } from "react-dom/server";

import HomePage from "@/app/page";
import PresumptivePage from "@/app/presumptive/page";
import PrivacyPage from "@/app/privacy/page";
import SecondaryLandingPage from "@/app/secondary/page";
import TermsPage from "@/app/terms/page";

function renderPageHtml(element: ReturnType<typeof HomePage>): string {
  return renderToStaticMarkup(element);
}

function expectSingleMainLandmark(html: string): void {
  const mainCount = (html.match(/<main/g) ?? []).length;
  expect(mainCount).toBe(1);
}

function expectHeadingHierarchy(html: string): void {
  expect(html).toContain("<h1");
  expect(html.includes("<h3") && !html.includes("<h2")).toBe(false);
}

describe("accessibility and UX content contracts", () => {
  it("renders one main landmark and a top-level heading on primary routes", () => {
    const pages = [
      renderPageHtml(HomePage()),
      renderPageHtml(SecondaryLandingPage()),
      renderPageHtml(PresumptivePage()),
      renderPageHtml(PrivacyPage()),
      renderPageHtml(TermsPage()),
    ];

    for (const page of pages) {
      expectSingleMainLandmark(page);
      expectHeadingHierarchy(page);
    }
  });

  it("keeps route copy explicit about non-diagnostic/non-deterministic scope", () => {
    const home = renderPageHtml(HomePage());
    const terms = renderPageHtml(TermsPage());
    const privacy = renderPageHtml(PrivacyPage());

    expect(home).toContain("This is a research aid, not advice.");
    expect(terms).toContain("Not Legal or Medical Advice");
    expect(privacy).toContain("Zero PII Policy");
  });

  it("keeps both legal routes discoverable from the main chooser page", () => {
    const home = renderPageHtml(HomePage());

    expect(home).toContain('href="/privacy"');
    expect(home).toContain('href="/terms"');
  });
});

FILE: tests/catalog.test.ts
================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { loadConditionCatalog } from "@/lib/catalog/load";
import {
  findCondition,
  groupByBodySystem,
  indexById,
  normalizeConditionName,
} from "@/lib/catalog/lookup";

const catalog = loadConditionCatalog();

describe("condition catalog", () => {
  it("loads every body system file", () => {
    expect(catalog.length).toBeGreaterThan(150);
  });

  it("uses globally unique ids", () => {
    const ids = catalog.map((c) => c.id);
    expect(new Set(ids).size).toBe(ids.length);
  });

  it("assigns every condition a body system", () => {
    for (const condition of catalog) {
      expect(condition.bodySystem.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    }
  });

  it("does not mark any rating citation verified without human review", () => {
    const verified = catalog.flatMap((condition) =>
      condition.ratingSchedule.filter((reference) => reference.verified),
    );
    expect(verified).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("records a review note whenever a rating citation is missing", () => {
    // Never invent a citation. An entry without one must say why.
    for (const condition of catalog) {
      if (condition.ratingSchedule.length === 0) {
        expect(condition.reviewNotes.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      }
    }
  });

  it("formats every rating citation consistently", () => {
    for (const condition of catalog) {
      for (const reference of condition.ratingSchedule) {
        expect(reference.citation).toMatch(/^38 CFR 4\.\d+[a-z]?$/);
        expect(reference.note.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      }
    }
  });

  it("never reuses an alias across two conditions", () => {
    const seen = new Map<string, string>();
    for (const condition of catalog) {
      for (const alias of condition.aliases) {
        const key = normalizeConditionName(alias);
        const previous = seen.get(key);
        expect(
          previous,
          `alias "${alias}" is claimed by both ${previous} and ${condition.id}`,
        ).toBeUndefined();
        seen.set(key, condition.id);
      }
    }
  });

  it("never lets an alias collide with another condition's name or id", () => {
    const names = new Map(
      catalog.map((c) => [normalizeConditionName(c.name), c.id]),
    );
    const ids = new Map(catalog.map((c) => [c.id, c.id]));
    for (const condition of catalog) {
      for (const alias of condition.aliases) {
        const key = normalizeConditionName(alias);
        const byName = names.get(key);
        if (byName) expect(byName).toBe(condition.id);
        const byId = ids.get(key.replace(/\s+/g, "-"));
        if (byId) expect(byId).toBe(condition.id);
      }
    }
  });
});

describe("findCondition", () => {
  it("resolves by name, id, and alias", () => {
    expect(findCondition(catalog, "Tinnitus")?.id).toBe("tinnitus");
    expect(findCondition(catalog, "ptsd")?.id).toBe("ptsd");
    expect(findCondition(catalog, "  PTSD  ")?.id).toBe("ptsd");
    expect(findCondition(catalog, "diabetes-mellitus-type-2")?.id).toBe(
      "diabetes-mellitus-type-2",
    );
  });

  it("returns null rather than guessing on partial input", () => {
    expect(findCondition(catalog, "knee")).toBeNull();
    expect(findCondition(catalog, "")).toBeNull();
    expect(findCondition(catalog, "not a real condition")).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("indexById and groupByBodySystem", () => {
  it("indexes every condition", () => {
    expect(indexById(catalog).size).toBe(catalog.length);
  });

  it("groups without losing or duplicating a condition", () => {
    const groups = groupByBodySystem(catalog);
    const total = groups.reduce((sum, [, items]) => sum + items.length, 0);
    expect(total).toBe(catalog.length);
    expect(groups.length).toBeGreaterThan(5);
  });
});

const kb = loadKnowledgeBase();
const draftPrimaryIds = new Set(draftMappings(kb).map((mapping) => mapping.id));

  it("ships at least three scenarios", () => {
    expect(scenarios.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3);

    "runs %s end to end without manual fixes",

      for (const recommendation of result.recommendations) {
        expect(recommendation.contributingPrimaries[0].rationale).toBeTruthy();
        expect(recommendation.sourceReferences.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
        for (const primary of recommendation.contributingPrimaries) {
          expect(draftPrimaryIds.has(primary.id)).toBe(false);
        }
      }
    },
  );
});

FILE: tests/event-log-privacy.test.ts

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { sanitizeAndValidateEventPayload } from '../src/lib/events/privacy-guard';

describe('Event Logger Privacy & Schema Evolution Suite', () => {
  it('detects and strips prohibited intake free text and PII fields', () => {
    const maliciousPayload = {
      eventType: 'WIZARD_STEP_VIEW',
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      freeText: 'Patient reports severe lumbar pain radiating down left leg',
      ssn: '123-45-6789',
      metadata: {
        rawNotes: 'Confidential doctor notes here',
        step: 2
      }
    };

    const result = sanitizeAndValidateEventPayload(maliciousPayload);

    expect(result.isValid).toBe(false);
    expect(result.violations).toContain('freeText');
    expect(result.violations).toContain('ssn');
    expect(result.violations).toContain('metadata.rawNotes');

    // Ensure sanitized payload has zero persistence of stripped fields
    expect(result.sanitizedPayload.freeText).toBeUndefined();
    expect(result.sanitizedPayload.ssn).toBeUndefined();
    expect((result.sanitizedPayload.metadata as Record<string, unknown>)?.rawNotes).toBeUndefined();
    expect(result.sanitizedPayload.metadata?.step).toBe(2);
  });

  it('allows clean, non-prohibited event payloads', () => {
    const cleanPayload = {
      eventType: 'CONDITION_SELECTED',
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      metadata: {
        conditionId: 'sleep-apnea',
        category: 'respiratory'
      }
    };

    const result = sanitizeAndValidateEventPayload(cleanPayload);
    expect(result.isValid).toBe(true);
    expect(result.violations.length).toBe(0);
    expect(result.sanitizedPayload).toEqual(cleanPayload);
  });
});

FILE: tests/event-log.test.ts

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { createMemoryLogger } from "@/lib/log/memory";
import type { RecommendationEvent } from "@/lib/log/types";

function event(overrides: Partial<RecommendationEvent> = {}): RecommendationEvent {
  return {
    id: "evt_1",
    createdAt: new Date("2026-01-01T00:00:00Z").toISOString(),
    recognizedPrimaries: ["PTSD"],
    unrecognizedPrimaryCount: 0,
    recommendedConditions: ["Sleep apnea"],
    engineVersion: "phase-1",
    ...overrides,
  };
}

describe("memory event logger", () => {
  it("records events and returns the most recent first", () => {
    const logger = createMemoryLogger({ silent: true });
    logger.record(event({ id: "evt_1" }));
    logger.record(event({ id: "evt_2" }));

    expect(logger.recent(10).map((e) => e.id)).toEqual(["evt_2", "evt_1"]);
  });

  it("respects the requested limit", () => {
    const logger = createMemoryLogger({ silent: true });
    logger.record(event({ id: "a" }));
    logger.record(event({ id: "b" }));
    logger.record(event({ id: "c" }));

    expect(logger.recent(2)).toHaveLength(2);
  });
});

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { MEDICAL_DISCLAIMER, NEVER_DIAGNOSTIC_NOTICE, validateGuardrails } from '../src/lib/guardrails';

import path from 'path';

describe('Phase 1 Guardrails & Regression Suite', () => {
  it('enforces centralized legal and medical disclaimer copy', () => {
    expect(MEDICAL_DISCLAIMER).toContain('Educational guidance tool only');
    expect(NEVER_DIAGNOSTIC_NOTICE).toContain('never diagnostic');
  });

  it('detects and blocks forbidden diagnostic or guarantee phrases', () => {
    const badCopy = "Based on your records, you have been diagnosed with PTSD and a guaranteed VA rating.";
    const check = validateGuardrails(badCopy);
    expect(check.isValid).toBe(false);
    expect(check.violations.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it('allows safe educational copy', () => {
    const safeCopy = "This platform is an informational estimate only and is never diagnostic.";
    const check = validateGuardrails(safeCopy);
    expect(check.isValid).toBe(true);
  });

  it('preserves data architecture separation between catalog, secondary, and presumptive routes', () => {
    const srcDir = path.join(process.cwd(), 'src', 'app');
    const hasCatalog = fs.existsSync(path.join(srcDir, 'scenarios')) || fs.existsSync(path.join(srcDir, 'results'));
    const hasSecondary = fs.existsSync(path.join(srcDir, 'secondary'));
    const hasPresumptive = fs.existsSync(path.join(srcDir, 'presumptive'));

    expect(hasCatalog).toBe(true);
    expect(hasSecondary).toBe(true);
    expect(hasPresumptive).toBe(true);
  });
});

    expect(result.success).toBe(false);
    expect(result.fileCount).toBe(0);

  });
});


================================================================================
FILE: tests/kb-integrity.test.ts
================================================================================

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';

interface SourceReference {
  citation: string;
  verified: boolean;
}

interface Suggestion {
  conditionId: string;
  rank: number;
  evidenceGaps: string[];
  sourceReferences?: SourceReference[];
}

interface KBFileSchema {
  draft?: boolean;
  suggestions?: Suggestion[];
}

describe('Knowledge Base Cross-File Integrity & Quality Gates', () => {
  const kbDir = path.join(process.cwd(), 'data', 'knowledge-base');
  const files = fs.readdirSync(kbDir).filter((f: string) => f.endsWith('.json'));

  it('validates cross-file invariants, rank continuity, and placeholder limits', () => {
    files.forEach((file: string) => {
      const filePath = path.join(kbDir, file);
      const content = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf8');
      const data: KBFileSchema = JSON.parse(content);
      const conditionId = file.replace('.json', '');

      // If marked as published/non-draft, strictly forbid placeholder strings
      if (data.draft === false) {
        expect(content).not.toContain('TODO');
        expect(content).not.toContain('TBD');
      }

      if (data.suggestions && Array.isArray(data.suggestions)) {
        const seenTargets = new Set<string>();
        
        data.suggestions.forEach((s: Suggestion, idx: number) => {
          // 1. Non-self reference check
          expect(s.conditionId).not.toBe(conditionId);

          // 2. Rank continuity & gapless sequence check (1, 2, 3...)
          expect(s.rank).toBe(idx + 1);

          // 3. No duplicate suggestion targets across the same condition
          expect(seenTargets.has(s.conditionId)).toBe(false);
          seenTargets.add(s.conditionId);

          // 4. Evidence gap depth check (must provide at least one concrete gap)
          expect(Array.isArray(s.evidenceGaps)).toBe(true);
          expect(s.evidenceGaps.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);

          // 5. Source references citation and unverified flag check
          if (s.sourceReferences && Array.isArray(s.sourceReferences)) {
            s.sourceReferences.forEach((ref: SourceReference) => {
              expect(ref.citation).toBeDefined();
              expect(typeof ref.verified).toBe('boolean');
            });
          }
        });
      }
    });
  });
});


================================================================================
FILE: tests/knowledge-base.test.ts
================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { loadConditionCatalog } from "@/lib/catalog/load";
import { indexById } from "@/lib/catalog/lookup";
import { conditionCatalogSchema } from "@/lib/catalog/schema";
import { loadKnowledgeBase } from "@/lib/kb/load";
import { draftMappings, mappedConditionIds } from "@/lib/kb/lookup";
import { knowledgeBaseSchema } from "@/lib/kb/schema";
import { validateKnowledgeBaseAgainstCatalog } from "@/lib/kb/validate";

const catalog = loadConditionCatalog();
const kb = loadKnowledgeBase();
const byId = indexById(catalog);

describe("referential integrity between mappings and the catalog", () => {
  it("resolves every mapping id to a catalog condition", () => {
    for (const mapping of kb) {
      expect(byId.has(mapping.id), `mapping "${mapping.id}" has no catalog entry`).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it("resolves every suggestion to a catalog condition", () => {
    for (const mapping of kb) {
      for (const suggestion of mapping.suggestions) {
        expect(
          byId.has(suggestion.conditionId),
          `"${mapping.id}" suggests unknown condition "${suggestion.conditionId}"`,
        ).toBe(true);
      }
    }
  });
});

describe("mapping integrity", () => {
  it("uses unique mapping ids", () => {
    const ids = kb.map((m) => m.id);
    expect(new Set(ids).size).toBe(ids.length);
  });

  it("gives every suggestion a rationale, an evidence gap, and a citation", () => {
    for (const mapping of kb) {
      for (const suggestion of mapping.suggestions) {
        expect(suggestion.rationale.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
        expect(suggestion.evidenceGaps.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
        expect(suggestion.sourceReferences.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
        for (const reference of suggestion.sourceReferences) {
          expect(reference.citation.trim().length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
          expect(reference.verified).toBe(false);
        }
      }
    }
  });

  it("uses unique, gapless ranks within each mapping", () => {
    for (const mapping of kb) {
      const ranks = mapping.suggestions.map((s) => s.rank).sort((a, b) => a - b);
      expect(ranks).toEqual(ranks.map((_, index) => index + 1));
    }
  });

  it("never suggests a condition as a secondary of itself", () => {
    for (const mapping of kb) {
      expect(mapping.suggestions.map((s) => s.conditionId)).not.toContain(mapping.id);
    }
  });

  it("never duplicates a conditionId within one mapping", () => {
    for (const mapping of kb) {
      const ids = mapping.suggestions.map((s) => s.conditionId);
      expect(new Set(ids).size).toBe(ids.length);
    }
  });

  it("does not mark any citation verified without human review", () => {
    const verified = kb.flatMap((mapping) =>
      mapping.suggestions.flatMap((suggestion) =>
        suggestion.sourceReferences.filter((reference) => reference.verified),
      ),
    );
    expect(verified).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("keeps published mappings free of placeholder text", () => {
    const authored = kb
      .filter((m) => !m.draft)
      .flatMap((mapping) =>
        mapping.suggestions.flatMap((suggestion) => [
          suggestion.rationale,
          ...suggestion.evidenceGaps,
          ...suggestion.sourceReferences.flatMap((r) => [r.citation, r.note]),
        ]),
      );
    for (const text of authored) {
      expect(text).not.toMatch(/TODO|FIXME|placeholder|lorem ipsum|TBD/i);
    }
  });
});

describe("draft mappings", () => {
  it("gives every draft a todo checklist and keeps it out of the published set", () => {
    const published = mappedConditionIds(kb);
    for (const draft of draftMappings(kb)) {
      expect(draft.todo.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(published.has(draft.id)).toBe(false);
    }
  });
});

describe("coverage", () => {
  it("reports how much of the catalog has published mappings", () => {
    const mapped = mappedConditionIds(kb);
    // Not an assertion about the target, just a guard that the relationship
    // stays sane: you cannot have more mappings than catalog conditions.
    expect(mapped.size).toBeLessThanOrEqual(catalog.length);
    expect(mapped.size).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
});

describe("validateKnowledgeBaseAgainstCatalog", () => {
  it("throws when a mapping id is not in the catalog", () => {
    const catalog = conditionCatalogSchema.parse([
      {
        id: "known-primary",
        name: "Known Primary",
        aliases: [],
        bodySystem: "Test",
        ratingSchedule: [],
        reviewNotes: ["test fixture"],
      },
      {
        id: "known-secondary",
        name: "Known Secondary",
        aliases: [],
        bodySystem: "Test",
        ratingSchedule: [],
        reviewNotes: ["test fixture"],
      },
    ]);
    const kb = knowledgeBaseSchema.parse([
      {
        id: "missing-primary",
        draft: false,
        todo: [],
        suggestions: [
          {
            conditionId: "known-secondary",
            rank: 1,
            prevalence: "commonly-explored",
            rationale: "test",
            evidenceGaps: ["test"],
            sourceReferences: [{ citation: "test", note: "test", verified: false }],
          },
        ],
      },
    ]);

    expect(() => validateKnowledgeBaseAgainstCatalog(catalog, kb)).toThrow(
      'Mapping "missing-primary" has no matching condition in data/conditions.',
    );
  });

  it("throws when a suggestion id is not in the catalog", () => {
    const catalog = conditionCatalogSchema.parse([
      {
        id: "known-primary",
        name: "Known Primary",
        aliases: [],
        bodySystem: "Test",
        ratingSchedule: [],
        reviewNotes: ["test fixture"],
      },
    ]);
    const kb = knowledgeBaseSchema.parse([
      {
        id: "known-primary",
        draft: false,
        todo: [],
        suggestions: [
          {
            conditionId: "missing-secondary",
            rank: 1,
            prevalence: "commonly-explored",
            rationale: "test",
            evidenceGaps: ["test"],
            sourceReferences: [{ citation: "test", note: "test", verified: false }],
          },
        ],
      },
    ]);

    expect(() => validateKnowledgeBaseAgainstCatalog(catalog, kb)).toThrow(
      'Mapping "known-primary" suggests unknown condition "missing-secondary".',
    );
  });
});

FILE: tests/presumptive.test.ts

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { loadExposureCatalog } from "@/lib/presumptive/load";
import { matchPresumptives } from "@/lib/presumptive/match";
import { exposureCategorySchema, presumptiveSourceSchema } from "@/lib/presumptive/schema";

describe("presumptive source validation", () => {
  it("rejects source references marked verified true", () => {
    const result = presumptiveSourceSchema.safeParse({
      citation: "VA source",
      note: "Human review required",
      url: "https://www.va.gov/",
      verified: true,
    });
    expect(result.success).toBe(false);
  });

  it("loads every shipped category with unverified source references", () => {
    const catalog = loadExposureCatalog();
    expect(catalog.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    for (const category of catalog) {
      for (const source of category.sourceReferences) {
        expect(source.verified).toBe(false);
      }
    }
  });
});

describe("presumptive matching integration", () => {
  const catalog = loadExposureCatalog();

  it("matches burn pit service windows when location and dates overlap", () => {
    const result = matchPresumptives(catalog, {
      locations: ["Iraq"],
      serviceStart: "2006-01-01",
      serviceEnd: "2007-01-01",
    });

    expect(result.matches.some((match) => match.category.id === "burn-pits")).toBe(true);
  });

  it("keeps unmatched locations explicit", () => {
    const result = matchPresumptives(catalog, {
      locations: ["UnknownForwardBaseX"],
      serviceStart: "2008-01-01",
      serviceEnd: "2009-01-01",
    });

    expect(result.matches.length).toBe(0);
    expect(result.unmatchedLocations).toEqual(["UnknownForwardBaseX"]);
  });

  it("flags location-only matching when dates are omitted", () => {
    const result = matchPresumptives(catalog, {
      locations: ["Republic of Vietnam"],
      serviceStart: null,
      serviceEnd: null,
    });

    expect(result.hasUnconfirmedDates).toBe(true);
    expect(result.matches.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(
      result.matches.some((match) =>
        match.matchedPeriods.some((period) => period.datesConfirmed === false),
      ),
    ).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe("presumptive category schema", () => {
  it("requires sourceCheckedOn and at least one source reference", () => {
    const result = exposureCategorySchema.safeParse({
      id: "test-category",
      name: "Test Category",
      authority: "Authority",
      summary: "Summary",
      qualifyingService: [
        {
          id: "test-period",
          label: "Test period",
          locations: ["Test"],
          startDate: "2000-01-01",
          endDate: null,
          note: null,
        },
      ],
      conditions: [
        {
          conditionId: "test-condition",
          name: "Test condition",
          group: "Test group",
          qualifier: null,
        },
      ],
      sourceReferences: [],
    });

    expect(result.success).toBe(false);
  });
});


================================================================================
FILE: tests/recommend.test.ts
================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { loadConditionCatalog } from "@/lib/catalog/load";
import { conditionCatalogSchema } from "@/lib/catalog/schema";
import { loadKnowledgeBase } from "@/lib/kb/load";
import { knowledgeBaseSchema } from "@/lib/kb/schema";
import { recommend } from "@/lib/engine/recommend";
import { intakeSchema, type Intake } from "@/lib/engine/types";

const catalog = loadConditionCatalog();
const kb = loadKnowledgeBase();

function intake(partial: Partial<Intake> & Pick<Intake, "primaryConditions">): Intake {
  return intakeSchema.parse(partial);
}

describe("recommend", () => {
  it("ports the original prototype ordering for PTSD", () => {
    const { recommendations } = recommend(
      catalog,
      kb,
      intake({ primaryConditions: [" PTSD "] }),
    );
    // Display names come from the catalog, not the mapping file.
    expect(recommendations.map((r) => r.conditionId)).toEqual([
      "sleep-apnea",
      "migraine-headaches",
      "erectile-dysfunction",
    ]);
    expect(recommendations[0].condition).toBe(
      "Sleep Apnea (Obstructive, Central, Mixed)",
    );
  });

  it("returns rationale, evidence gaps, and citations for every suggestion", () => {
    const { recommendations } = recommend(
      catalog,
      kb,
      intake({ primaryConditions: ["ptsd"] }),
    );
    for (const recommendation of recommendations) {
      expect(recommendation.contributingPrimaries.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(recommendation.contributingPrimaries[0].rationale).toBeTruthy();
      expect(recommendation.evidenceGaps.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(recommendation.sourceReferences.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    }
  });

  it("returns nothing for an unknown primary and reports it as unrecognized", () => {
    const result = recommend(
      catalog,
      kb,
      intake({ primaryConditions: ["unknown condition"] }),
    );
    expect(result.recommendations).toEqual([]);
    expect(result.unrecognizedPrimaries).toEqual(["unknown condition"]);
    expect(result.unmappedPrimaries).toEqual([]);
    expect(result.recognizedPrimaries).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("carries the body system through from the catalog", () => {
    const { recommendations } = recommend(
      catalog,
      kb,
      intake({ primaryConditions: ["ptsd"] }),
    );
    for (const recommendation of recommendations) {
      expect(recommendation.bodySystem.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    }
  });

  it("resolves a primary by alias as well as by name", () => {
    const byId = recommend(catalog, kb, intake({ primaryConditions: ["ptsd"] }));
    const byName = recommend(
      catalog,
      kb,
      intake({ primaryConditions: ["Post-Traumatic Stress Disorder (PTSD)"] }),
    );
    expect(byName.recommendations).toEqual(byId.recommendations);
  });

  it("separates mapped, unmapped, and unrecognized primaries", () => {
    const result = recommend(
      catalog,
      kb,
      intake({
        primaryConditions: ["Tinnitus", "Plantar Fasciitis", "not a condition"],
      }),
    );
    // Plantar fasciitis is a real catalog condition with no authored mappings.
    // That is different from input we could not resolve at all, and the user
    // deserves to be told which is which.
    expect(result.recognizedPrimaries).toEqual(["Tinnitus"]);
    expect(result.unmappedPrimaries).toEqual(["Plantar Fasciitis"]);
    expect(result.unrecognizedPrimaries).toEqual(["not a condition"]);
    expect(result.recommendations.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it("merges a suggestion shared by two primaries and ranks it above single-source peers", () => {
    const result = recommend(
      catalog,
      kb,
      intake({ primaryConditions: ["Tinnitus", "Lumbar strain"] }),
    );
    const depression = result.recommendations.find(
      (r) => r.conditionId === "depressive-disorder",
    );

    // Tinnitus rank 3 (0.333) + lumbar strain rank 3 (0.333) = 0.667, which is
    // above either contribution alone.
    expect(depression).toBeDefined();
    expect(depression!.contributingPrimaries).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(depression!.contributingPrimaries.map((p) => p.name)).toEqual([
      "Lumbar Strain",
      "Tinnitus",
    ]);
    expect(depression!.score).toBeGreaterThan(1 / 3);
  });

  it("does not suggest a condition the user already listed as a primary", () => {
    // Knee injury lists lumbar strain as a compensatory secondary. If the user
    // is already service connected for lumbar strain, suggesting it is noise.
    const kneeOnly = recommend(catalog, kb, intake({ primaryConditions: ["Knee injury"] }));
    expect(kneeOnly.recommendations.map((r) => r.conditionId)).toContain(
      "lumbar-strain",
    );

    const both = recommend(
      catalog,
      kb,
      intake({ primaryConditions: ["Knee injury", "Lumbar strain"] }),
    );
    expect(both.recommendations.map((r) => r.conditionId)).not.toContain(
      "lumbar-strain",
    );
  });

  it("deduplicates repeated primary conditions", () => {
    const single = recommend(catalog, kb, intake({ primaryConditions: ["PTSD"] }));
    const doubled = recommend(
      catalog,
      kb,
      intake({ primaryConditions: ["PTSD", "ptsd", " PTSD "] }),
    );
    expect(doubled.recognizedPrimaries).toEqual([
      "Post-Traumatic Stress Disorder (PTSD)",
    ]);
    expect(doubled.recommendations).toEqual(single.recommendations);
  });

  it("sorts strictly by descending score", () => {
    const { recommendations } = recommend(
      catalog,
      kb,
      intake({ primaryConditions: ["Knee injury", "Lumbar strain"] }),
    );
    const scores = recommendations.map((r) => r.score);
    expect(scores).toEqual([...scores].sort((a, b) => b - a));
  });

  it("surfaces matching intake context without changing the ranking", () => {
    const base = recommend(catalog, kb, intake({ primaryConditions: ["Tinnitus"] }));
    const withContext = recommend(
      catalog,
      kb,
      intake({ primaryConditions: ["Tinnitus"], symptoms: ["anxiety"] }),
    );

    expect(withContext.recommendations.map((r) => r.condition)).toEqual(
      base.recommendations.map((r) => r.condition),
    );
    expect(withContext.recommendations.map((r) => r.score)).toEqual(
      base.recommendations.map((r) => r.score),
    );

    const anxiety = withContext.recommendations.find(
      (r) => r.conditionId === "anxiety-disorder",
    );
    expect(anxiety?.matchedIntakeContext).toContain("anxiety");
  });

  it("never emits a numeric confidence, only a qualitative prevalence band", () => {
    const { recommendations } = recommend(
      catalog,
      kb,
      intake({ primaryConditions: ["Diabetes mellitus type II"] }),
    );
    for (const recommendation of recommendations) {
      expect([
        "commonly-explored",
        "sometimes-explored",
        "occasionally-explored",
      ]).toContain(recommendation.prevalence);
    }
  });

  it("ignores draft mappings when building recommendations", () => {
    const syntheticCatalog = conditionCatalogSchema.parse([
      {
        id: "primary-condition",
        name: "Primary Condition",
        aliases: [],
        bodySystem: "Test",
        ratingSchedule: [],
        reviewNotes: ["test fixture"],
      },
      {
        id: "secondary-condition",
        name: "Secondary Condition",
        aliases: [],
        bodySystem: "Test",
        ratingSchedule: [],
        reviewNotes: ["test fixture"],
      },
    ]);
    const syntheticKb = knowledgeBaseSchema.parse([
      {
        id: "primary-condition",
        draft: true,
        todo: ["human review pending"],
        suggestions: [
          {
            conditionId: "secondary-condition",
            rank: 1,
            prevalence: "commonly-explored",
            rationale: "draft only",
            evidenceGaps: ["draft only"],
            sourceReferences: [{ citation: "test", note: "test", verified: false }],
          },
        ],
      },
    ]);

    const result = recommend(
      syntheticCatalog,
      syntheticKb,
      intake({ primaryConditions: ["primary-condition"] }),
    );

    expect(result.recommendations).toEqual([]);
    expect(result.recognizedPrimaries).toEqual([]);
    expect(result.unmappedPrimaries).toEqual(["Primary Condition"]);
  });
});

describe("intakeSchema", () => {
  it("requires at least one primary condition", () => {
    expect(() => intakeSchema.parse({ primaryConditions: [] })).toThrow();
  });

  it("defaults the optional intake fields", () => {
    const parsed = intakeSchema.parse({ primaryConditions: ["PTSD"] });
    expect(parsed.symptoms).toEqual([]);
    expect(parsed.medications).toEqual([]);
    expect(parsed.exposures).toEqual([]);
    expect(parsed.notes).toBe("");
  });
});

FILE: tests/secondary-wizard-routes.test.ts

import React from "react";
import { renderToStaticMarkup } from "react-dom/server";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

import RatingsStepPage from "@/app/secondary/ratings/page";
import SuggestionsStepPage from "@/app/secondary/suggestions/page";
import UploadStepPage from "@/app/secondary/upload/page";

vi.mock("next/link", () => ({
  default: ({
    href,
    children,
    className,
  }: {
    href: string;
    children: React.ReactNode;
    className?: string;
  }) => React.createElement("a", { href, className }, children),
}));

describe("secondary wizard route progression", () => {
  it("keeps prior query state in ratings back navigation", async () => {
    const html = renderToStaticMarkup(
      await RatingsStepPage({
        searchParams: Promise.resolve({
          diagnosed: ["ptsd", "tinnitus"],
          serviceConnected: ["ptsd"],
        }),
      }),
    );

    expect(html).toContain(
      'href="/secondary/service-connected?diagnosed=ptsd&amp;diagnosed=tinnitus&amp;serviceConnected=ptsd"',
    );
  });

  it("keeps prior query state in upload back navigation", async () => {
    const html = renderToStaticMarkup(
      await UploadStepPage({
        searchParams: Promise.resolve({
          diagnosed: ["ptsd", "tinnitus"],
          serviceConnected: ["ptsd", "tinnitus"],
          "rating-ptsd": "70",
          "rating-tinnitus": "decline",
        }),
      }),
    );

    expect(html).toContain(
      "href=\"/secondary/ratings?diagnosed=ptsd&amp;diagnosed=tinnitus&amp;serviceConnected=ptsd&amp;serviceConnected=tinnitus&amp;rating-ptsd=70&amp;rating-tinnitus=decline\"",
    );
  });

  it("prefills prior rating selections when returning to ratings", async () => {
    const html = renderToStaticMarkup(
      await RatingsStepPage({
        searchParams: Promise.resolve({
          diagnosed: ["ptsd", "tinnitus"],
          serviceConnected: ["ptsd", "tinnitus"],
          "rating-ptsd": "70",
          "rating-tinnitus": "decline",
        }),
      }),
    );

    expect(html).toMatch(
      /name="rating-ptsd"[\s\S]*<option value="70" selected="">70%<\/option>/,
    );
    expect(html).toMatch(
      /name="rating-tinnitus"[\s\S]*<option value="decline" selected="">I prefer not to answer<\/option>/,
    );
  });

  it("prefills prior upload selection when returning to upload", async () => {
    const html = renderToStaticMarkup(
      await UploadStepPage({
        searchParams: Promise.resolve({
          diagnosed: ["ptsd"],
          serviceConnected: ["ptsd"],
          "rating-ptsd": "70",
          documentsShared: "yes",
        }),
      }),
    );

    expect(html).toContain('name="documentsShared" checked="" value="yes"');
    expect(html).not.toContain('name="documentsShared" checked="" value="no"');
  });

  it("blocks suggestions step when upload selection is missing", async () => {
    const html = renderToStaticMarkup(
      await SuggestionsStepPage({
        searchParams: Promise.resolve({
          diagnosed: ["ptsd"],
          serviceConnected: ["ptsd"],
          "rating-ptsd": "70",
        }),
      }),
    );

    expect(html).toContain("Complete prior steps first");
    expect(html).toContain("Back to optional document upload");
    expect(html).toContain(
      'href="/secondary/upload?diagnosed=ptsd&amp;serviceConnected=ptsd&amp;rating-ptsd=70"',
    );
  });
});


================================================================================
FILE: tests/secondary-workflow.test.ts
================================================================================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { conditionCatalogSchema } from "@/lib/catalog/schema";
import type { Recommendation } from "@/lib/engine/types";
import { groupSuggestionsByPrimary } from "@/lib/secondary-workflow/suggestions";
import {
  parseDiagnosedPrefill,
  parseDocumentsSelection,
  parseDiagnosedSelection,
  parseServiceConnectedPrefill,
  parseRatingsSelection,
  parseServiceConnectedSelection,
  parseWorkflowState,
  workflowStateToSearchParams,
} from "@/lib/secondary-workflow/state";

function toQueryParams(searchParams: URLSearchParams): Record<string, string | string[]> {
  const keys = new Set<string>();
  for (const [key] of searchParams.entries()) {
    keys.add(key);
  }

  const params: Record<string, string | string[]> = {};
  for (const key of keys) {
    const values = searchParams.getAll(key);
    params[key] = values.length === 1 ? values[0] : values;
  }
  return params;
}

const catalog = conditionCatalogSchema.parse([
  {
    id: "ptsd",
    name: "Post-Traumatic Stress Disorder (PTSD)",
    aliases: ["post-traumatic stress disorder"],
    bodySystem: "Mental Disorders",
    ratingSchedule: [],
    reviewNotes: ["test fixture"],
  },
  {
    id: "tinnitus",
    name: "Tinnitus",
    aliases: [],
    bodySystem: "Auditory",
    ratingSchedule: [],
    reviewNotes: ["test fixture"],
  },
  {
    id: "sleep-apnea",
    name: "Sleep Apnea",
    aliases: [],
    bodySystem: "Respiratory",
    ratingSchedule: [],
    reviewNotes: ["test fixture"],
  },
]);

describe("secondary workflow state parsing", () => {
  it("requires at least one diagnosed condition", () => {
    expect(() => parseDiagnosedSelection({}, catalog)).toThrow();
  });

  it("keeps diagnosed selections limited to catalog ids", () => {
    const prefill = parseDiagnosedPrefill(
      { diagnosed: ["ptsd", "unknown", "tinnitus"] },
      catalog,
    );
    expect(prefill).toEqual(["ptsd", "tinnitus"]);
    expect(parseDiagnosedSelection({ diagnosed: prefill }, catalog).diagnosedConditionIds).toEqual([
      "ptsd",
      "tinnitus",
    ]);
  });

  it("prefills service-connected selections when present", () => {
    const parsed = parseServiceConnectedPrefill(
      {
        diagnosed: ["ptsd", "tinnitus"],
        serviceConnected: ["ptsd"],
      },
      catalog,
    );

    expect(parsed).toEqual({
      diagnosedConditionIds: ["ptsd", "tinnitus"],
      serviceConnectedConditionIds: ["ptsd"],
    });
  });

  it("accepts service-connected status only for diagnosed conditions", () => {
    const parsed = parseServiceConnectedSelection(
      {
        diagnosed: ["ptsd", "tinnitus"],
        "serviceConnected-ptsd": "yes",
        "serviceConnected-tinnitus": "no",
      },
      catalog,
    );
    expect(parsed.serviceConnectedConditionIds).toEqual(["ptsd"]);
  });

  it("requires at least one service-connected condition", () => {
    expect(() =>
      parseServiceConnectedSelection(
        {
          diagnosed: ["ptsd"],
          "serviceConnected-ptsd": "no",
        },
        catalog,
      ),
    ).toThrow();
  });

  it("parses ratings as 0-100 integers or decline", () => {
    const parsed = parseRatingsSelection(
      {
        diagnosed: ["ptsd", "tinnitus"],
        serviceConnected: ["ptsd", "tinnitus"],
        "rating-ptsd": "0",
        "rating-tinnitus": "decline",
      },
      catalog,
    );

    expect(parsed.ratingsByConditionId).toEqual({
      ptsd: 0,
      tinnitus: null,
    });
  });

  it("rejects rating values outside 0 through 100", () => {
    expect(() =>
      parseRatingsSelection(
        {
          diagnosed: ["ptsd"],
          serviceConnected: ["ptsd"],
          "rating-ptsd": "120",
        },
        catalog,
      ),
    ).toThrow();
  });

  it("round-trips full workflow state through query params", () => {
    const initial = {
      diagnosedConditionIds: ["ptsd", "tinnitus"],
      serviceConnectedConditionIds: ["ptsd"],
      ratingsByConditionId: { ptsd: 70 },
      documentsShared: true,
    };

    const parsed = parseWorkflowState(
      toQueryParams(workflowStateToSearchParams(initial)),
      catalog,
    );

    expect(parsed).toEqual(initial);
  });

  it("requires upload-step selection before suggestions/evidence", () => {
    expect(() =>
      parseDocumentsSelection(
        {
          diagnosed: ["ptsd"],
          serviceConnected: ["ptsd"],
          "rating-ptsd": "70",
        },
        catalog,
      ),
    ).toThrow();
  });
});

describe("groupSuggestionsByPrimary", () => {
  it("groups suggestions under each selected service-connected primary", () => {
    const recommendations: Recommendation[] = [
      {
        conditionId: "sleep-apnea",
        condition: "Sleep Apnea",
        bodySystem: "Respiratory",
        prevalence: "commonly-explored",
        score: 1,
        contributingPrimaries: [
          { id: "ptsd", name: "Post-Traumatic Stress Disorder (PTSD)", rank: 1, rationale: "r1" },
          { id: "tinnitus", name: "Tinnitus", rank: 2, rationale: "r2" },
        ],
        evidenceGaps: ["gap"],
        sourceReferences: [{ citation: "38 CFR 3.310", note: "test", verified: false }],
        matchedIntakeContext: [],
      },
    ];

    const grouped = groupSuggestionsByPrimary(
      catalog,
      ["ptsd", "tinnitus"],
      recommendations,
    );

    expect(grouped).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(grouped[0].recommendations).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(grouped[1].recommendations).toHaveLength(1);
  });
});


================================================================================
FILE: tests/source-count-policy.test.ts
================================================================================

import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import { suggestionSchema } from "../src/lib/kb/schema";

describe("P1 Ticket 6: Source-Count Policy Enforcement (1 to 3 Sources)", () => {
  const validSource = {
    citation: "38 CFR § 3.310",
    note: "Direct secondary connection authority.",
    verified: false as const
  };

  it("allows suggestions with exactly 1, 2, or 3 source references", () => {
    // 1 Source
    const result1 = suggestionSchema.safeParse({
      conditionId: "radiculopathy",
      rank: 1,
      prevalence: "commonly-explored",
      rationale: "Nerve irritation.",
      evidenceGaps: ["Diagnosis"],
      sourceReferences: [validSource]
    });
    expect(result1.success).toBe(true);

    // 3 Sources (Maximum allowed)
    const result3 = suggestionSchema.safeParse({
      conditionId: "radiculopathy",
      rank: 1,
      prevalence: "commonly-explored",
      rationale: "Nerve irritation.",
      evidenceGaps: ["Diagnosis"],
      sourceReferences: [validSource, validSource, validSource]
    });
    expect(result3.success).toBe(true);
  });

  it("rejects suggestions with zero source references (below minimum)", () => {
    const result = suggestionSchema.safeParse({
      conditionId: "radiculopathy",
      rank: 1,
      prevalence: "commonly-explored",
      rationale: "Nerve irritation.",
      evidenceGaps: ["Diagnosis"],
      sourceReferences: []
    });
    expect(result.success).toBe(false);
  });

  it("rejects suggestions exceeding 4 or more source references (above maximum)", () => {
    const result = suggestionSchema.safeParse({
      conditionId: "radiculopathy",
      rank: 1,
      prevalence: "commonly-explored",
      rationale: "Nerve irritation.",
      evidenceGaps: ["Diagnosis"],
      sourceReferences: [validSource, validSource, validSource, validSource]
    });
    expect(result.success).toBe(false);
  });
});

import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";

describe("P2 Ticket 7: Upload Step Product Contract", () => {
  it("defines strict non-retentive privacy rules on the upload route", () => {
    const pagePath = path.join(process.cwd(), "src/app/secondary/upload/page.tsx");
    expect(fs.existsSync(pagePath)).toBe(true);

    const content = fs.readFileSync(pagePath, "utf8");
    expect(content).toContain("Evidence Upload Privacy Contract");
    expect(content).toContain("strictly optional");
    expect(content).toContain("raw text logs");
  });
});

FILE: tests/va-adapter-resilience.test.ts

import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import { fetchSandboxVABits } from "../src/lib/va";

describe("Phase 2 VA Adapter Failure-Mode & Resilience Suite", () => {
  it("handles feature flag disabled state gracefully", async () => {
    const result = await fetchSandboxVABits("user-123", { enabled: false, timeoutMs: 1000 });
    expect(result.success).toBe(false);
    expect(result.error).toContain("disabled");
  });

  it("recovers gracefully from simulated API timeouts", async () => {
    const result = await fetchSandboxVABits("user-123", { enabled: true, timeoutMs: 10, mockFailureMode: "timeout" });
    expect(result.success).toBe(false);
    expect(result.error).toBeDefined();
  });

  it("recovers gracefully from simulated upstream server errors (500)", async () => {
    const result = await fetchSandboxVABits("user-123", { enabled: true, timeoutMs: 1000, mockFailureMode: "error" });
    expect(result.success).toBe(false);
    expect(result.error).toContain("Internal Server Error");
  });

  it("successfully retrieves mock sandbox payload when operating normally", async () => {
    const result = await fetchSandboxVABits("user-123", { enabled: true, timeoutMs: 1000, mockFailureMode: "none" });
    expect(result.success).toBe(true);
    expect(result.data?.ratingPercentage).toBe(70);
  });
});


================================================================================
FILE: tests/workflow-constraints.test.ts
================================================================================

import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import { recommend } from "../src/lib/engine/recommend";
import type { ConditionCatalog } from "../src/lib/catalog/schema";
import type { KnowledgeBase } from "../src/lib/kb/schema";
import type { Recommendation } from "../src/lib/engine/types";

describe("P1 Ticket 5: Suggestion Constraints to Diagnosed Set", () => {
  const mockCatalog: ConditionCatalog = [
    { id: "lumbar-strain", name: "Lumbar Strain", bodySystem: "Musculoskeletal", aliases: [], ratingSchedule: [], reviewNotes: ["Verified"] },
    { id: "radiculopathy", name: "Radiculopathy", bodySystem: "Neurological", aliases: [], ratingSchedule: [], reviewNotes: ["Verified"] },
    { id: "depression", name: "Major Depressive Disorder", bodySystem: "Mental Health", aliases: [], ratingSchedule: [], reviewNotes: ["Verified"] }
  ];

  const mockKb: KnowledgeBase = [
    {
      id: "lumbar-strain",
      draft: false,
      todo: [],
      suggestions: [
        {
          conditionId: "radiculopathy",
          rank: 1,
          prevalence: "commonly-explored",
          rationale: "Nerve root compression due to lumbar spine pathology.",
          evidenceGaps: ["Current diagnosis"],
          sourceReferences: [{ citation: "38 CFR 3.310", note: "Direct secondary", verified: false }]
        },
        {
          conditionId: "depression",
          rank: 2,
          prevalence: "sometimes-explored",
          rationale: "Chronic pain leading to depressive symptoms.",
          evidenceGaps: ["Mental health diagnosis"],
          sourceReferences: [{ citation: "38 CFR 3.310", note: "Secondary pain effect", verified: false }]
        }
      ]
    }
  ];

  it("restricts recommendations strictly to user-selected diagnosed conditions", () => {
    const intake = {
      primaryConditions: ["Lumbar Strain"],
      diagnosedConditions: ["Radiculopathy"], // Depression is mapped, but NOT diagnosed by user
      symptoms: [],
      medications: [],
      exposures: [],
      notes: ""
    };

    const result = recommend(mockCatalog, mockKb, intake);
    
    // Should contain Radiculopathy, but filter out Depression
    expect(result.recommendations.map((r: Recommendation) => r.conditionId)).toContain("radiculopathy");
    expect(result.recommendations.map((r: Recommendation) => r.conditionId)).not.toContain("depression");
  });
});
